Each latent unit corresponds to a human-readable hyper-rectangle in feature space; an instance is encoded by how strongly it falls inside each interval relative to the other units, and its reconstruction error is the anomaly score. This keeps the power of differentiable representation learning while exposing an inspectable internal structure. We make the inductive bias precise: a certified reconstruction-error lower bound for points that fall outside every active coordinate of the learned support (with a Lipschitz-enforced decoder), and a graded, empirically verified suppression mechanism for the usual case in which only a few features are abnormal; and we provide a closed-form, label-free importance that ranks each (unit, feature) pair from quantities the model already maintains, turning trained intervals into auditable candidate constraints without ever seeing an anomaly label. On 48 ADBench benchmarks against 22 baselines under a common [-1, 1]-normalized protocol, DIFFINT attains the best mean rank overall on both metrics (4.10 on ROC-AUC, 4.16 on AUPR); among inlier-only detectors it leads its regime clearly, and it is competitive with the strongest contaminated-data detectors (see the stratified and complete-case analyses). It is the only interpretable detector in the statistically-tied leading cluster of seven methods.

</details>


### [248] [Beyond Endpoint Scores: Time- and Capacity-Conditioned Evaluation of Continual Knowledge Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03900)
*Heejin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出持续知识更新方法的评估若仅基于单一检查点和固定适配器秩是不充分的，发现方法优劣取决于评估时间和回放容量，提出应报告性能轨迹和容量扫描，而非单一赢家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常通过单一最终检查点和常规适配器秩来宣称某种持续更新方法的优越性，作者认为这种评估方式不足以识别真正的最佳操作点，可能导致错误的结论。

Method: 在固定周期性层级结构下，将其与累积回放方法在24个月的Wikidata数据流上进行对比，系统性地变化评估月份、回放LoRA秩以及查询表述，并在Qwen2.5-1.5B和Llama-3.2-1B模型及持留 paraphrases 上验证。

Result: 实验显示“赢家”随评估区域变化而反转：在Qwen2.5-1.5B上，周期性层级在秩-8时领先5.0分，但在秩-72时落后11.6分；高秩下时间平均回放领先9-13分。该现象在不同模型和查询变体中一致出现。

Conclusion: 持续更新中的方法排序 jointly 依赖于测量时间和回放侧适应容量；建议报告性能轨迹和容量扫描，仅在排序稳定时宣称赢家，否则应报告赢家区域和保留-稳定性-成本前沿；在此协议下，周期性层级是低更新成本的操作点，而非质量赢家。

Abstract: Continual knowledge-updating methods are often declared superior from one final checkpoint and one conventional adapter rank. We show that this can be insufficient to identify the better operating point. Holding a periodic hierarchy fixed, we compare it with cumulative replay over a 24-month Wikidata stream while varying evaluation month, replay LoRA rank, and query formulation. The apparent winner changes across this region: on Qwen2.5-1.5B, the hierarchy's 5.0-point advantage over rank-8 replay becomes an 11.6-point deficit against rank-72 replay, and at high ranks a consolidation-aligned endpoint can suggest a tie while time-averaged replay leads by 9-13 points. The same rank-conditioned reversal appears on Llama-3.2-1B and held-out paraphrases.
  These results show that method ranking in continual updating can depend jointly on when performance is measured and how much replay-side adaptation capacity the baseline receives. We therefore propose reporting trajectories and capacity sweeps, and declaring a robust winner only when the ordering is stable across the evaluation region; otherwise, comparisons should report winner regions and retention-stability-cost frontiers. Under this protocol, the periodic hierarchy is a lower-update-cost operating point, not a quality winner.

</details>


### [249] [RATL: Learning from Retrieved Residuals for Robust Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03937)
*Yuchen He,Yueyang Cang,Zhiyuan Ning,Ningyu Wang,Li Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RATL方法，通过检索历史预测残差而非目标值，结合反馈校正机制，提升多元时间序列预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检索增强生成（RAG）在连续输出回归中直接复用检索目标值不够鲁棒，且现有预测流程未将历史残差作为可访问的记忆用于推理。

Method: 冻结基础预测器构建检索键，将其历史预测残差构建为仅训练期可用的记忆；推理时检索相似历史上下文中的残差轨迹，通过集合感知路由器选择并组合这些轨迹进行反馈校正。

Result: 实验表明匹配当前上下文的历史残差包含可复用预测信息，RATL在多数实验设置中提升冻结基础预测器性能；消融实验证明学习路由增强原始残差反馈，基于验证的校正强度选择限制残差过注入。

Conclusion: RATL将检索对象从历史目标值转向基础模型特定的历史预测误差，为连续输出预测提供基于残差记忆的学习反馈校正范式。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) complements parametric models with retrieved external evidence. The same idea is attractive for continuous-output regression, but directly reusing retrieved target values is often not robust when samples differ in output level, numerical scale, or local dynamics. Moreover, conventional forecasting pipelines generally use residuals for model optimization and error diagnosis, but do not retain individual historical residual examples as memory that can be accessed at inference time.For multivariate time-series forecasting, we propose RATL, a plug-in residual-retrieval and feedback-correction method. RATL freezes a base forecaster to construct retrieval keys and turns its historical forecast residuals into a train-only memory specific to that base model. At inference time, RATL retrieves residual trajectories from similar historical contexts subject to causal availability constraints, then uses a set-aware router operating over forecast blocks and variables to select and combine these trajectories. Experiments show that historical residuals matched to the current context contain reusable forecasting information and that RATL improves frozen base forecasters in most experimental settings. Ablations further show that learned routing strengthens raw residual feedback, while validation-based correction-strength selection limits residual over-injection.On real-world benchmarks, we use iTransformer as the primary frozen base forecaster, compare against multiple strong forecasting baselines, and test transferability across backbones. The results show that RATL can further improve base-forecaster performance in most settings.Overall, RATL shifts the retrieved object from historical target values to base-model-specific historical forecast errors, providing a plug-in, residual-memory-based paradigm for learned feedback correction in continuous-output forecasting.

</details>


### [250] [Headroom-Drift Replay: A Primitive for Principled Replay Control in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03941)
*Hyun Bin Park,Du-Seong Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Headroom-Drift Replay 方法，通过分组级回放控制优化 RL 后训练，在不增加额外生成或训练开销的情况下，显著降低智能体设置中的时间成本并保持性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 RL 的推理模型后训练受限于重复的新 rollout 生成，尤其在环境交互主导时间成本的智能体场景中。现有回放方法通常嵌入复杂训练流程，难以隔离回放本身的贡献。

Method: 提出 Headroom-Drift Replay，一种针对 GRPO 的分组级回放控制原语。Headroom 按剩余学习价值对存储组排序，Drift 按与当前策略的兼容性进行门控。保持新鲜在线策略流不变，不添加辅助生成或训练机制。

Result: 在数学推理、多模态推理和智能体搜索基准测试中，该方法优于简单回放，在 Avg Mean@32 上匹配或超越更广泛的回放方法。在智能体搜索中，以显著更低的时间成本提供可比质量。

Conclusion: 原则性的回放选择本身即可显著提升效率，Headroom-Drift Replay 在降低墙钟时间的同时保持性能，为 RL 后训练提供了高效的回放控制方案。

Abstract: RL-based post-training for reasoning models is increasingly bottlenecked by repeated fresh rollout generation, particularly in agentic settings where environment interaction dominates wall-clock cost. Replay can reduce this burden by reusing past trajectories, but existing methods typically embed it within larger training pipelines involving exploration, experience restructuring, or mixed-policy optimization. This makes replay's own contribution difficult to isolate. We ask a focused question: how far can principled replay selection alone go? We introduce Headroom-Drift Replay, a group-level replay control primitive for GRPO that separates reuse into two decisions. Headroom ranks stored groups by remaining learning value, while Drift gates them by compatibility with the current policy. The fresh on-policy stream remains unchanged, and the method adds no auxiliary generation or training machinery. Across mathematical reasoning, multimodal reasoning, and Agentic Search benchmarks, this single intervention outperforms naive replay and matches or exceeds broader replay methods on Avg Mean@32. In Agentic Search, where environment interaction dominates cost, it delivers comparable quality at materially lower wall-clock time.

</details>


### [251] [VestigeKV: The NoPE-MLA KV Cache Carries Its Own Eviction Signal in a Vestigial Branch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03949)
*WenJie Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VestigeKV 提出了一种无需训练的 KV 缓存压缩方法，利用 NoPE 模型中 RoPE 遗留的 64 维解耦分支作为查询无关的显著性信号，将缓存划分为常驻显存的“关注层”和可精确检索的“归档层”，在 32 倍压缩下仍保持 0.92 的检索召回率，且该方法仅适用于无位置编码（NoPE）架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决长上下文场景中 KV 缓存需在查询发生前进行压缩的难题。现有的基于观测注意力（如 H2O, SnapKV）的方法在无位置编码（NoPE）模型中失效，因为在查询发生前无法观测到 token 的重要性，导致检索性能急剧下降（0.00-0.33）。

Method: 在 Kimi Linear 模型上，VestigeKV 利用 NoPE 训练重用途的 RoPE 遗留特征（64 维解耦分支）作为查询无关的显著性信号。该方法读取每行 11% 的数据，将缓存分区：顶部 m 行保留在“关注层”，其余行精确移至 GPU 驻留的“归档层”（非删除），并通过认证触发器按需检索。无需训练、量化或修改权重/内核。

Result: 在 8k 到 65k 上下文长度下，8 倍压缩时检索召回率为 1.00，32 倍压缩时为 0.92，与全行选择无差距。在 32 倍压缩下，关注层仅占每 token 缓存的 0.25 KB（原 8.1 KB）。标准配置下的召回层在 128 倍压缩下仍保持 1.00 召回率。相比之下，该方法在 RoPE 模型上失效（召回率降至 0.08）。

Conclusion: VestigeKV 证明了在无位置编码（NoPE）架构中，利用查询无关的显著性信号进行精确缓存压缩是可行的，且能实现极高的压缩比而不损失检索精度；同时指出在 RoPE 架构下，由于旋转特性的存在，查询无关的显著性信号稀疏且无法实现精确合并，因此该方法具有 NoPE 排他性。

Abstract: The problem. A long-lived KV cache must be compressed before the queries that will read it exist; selection by observed attention (H2O, SnapKV) collapses there (0.00-0.33 needle retrieval on a NoPE MLA model), because a token's importance has not yet been observed. The method. On Kimi Linear, VestigeKV evicts by a query-independent signal the cache already carries: the 64-dimensional decoupled branch, a vestige of RoPE that NoPE training repurposes into a salience channel. Reading 11% of each row, it partitions the cache: the top-m rows stay in the attended tier; every other row moves -- exactly, never deleted -- to a GPU-resident archive reachable per step by a certified trigger. No training, no quantization, no weight or kernel change. Cost. Nothing measurable: retrieval holds at 1.00 under 8x and 0.92 under 32x from 8k to 65k context, zero gap to full-row selection. The attended tier is 0.25 KB of Kimi Linear's 8.1 KB per-token cache at 32x; the archive stays bit-exact and GPU-resident, with host offload as the VRAM-reclaiming variant. The recall tier -- the standard configuration -- holds 128x at 1.00. Kimi K3 is reported to use a NoPE Gated-MLA variant; if its cache layout matches, the method plausibly extends there -- we make no claim beyond the measured model. NoPE exclusivity. The identical operator on a RoPE MLA collapses to 0.08 (plain eviction: 0.42); query-independent salience itself exists only without rotation (top-1 targets span 2.3-6.7% of tokens vs. 10.2-46.8%), and query-universal exact merging is provably impossible under RoPE. All thresholds were frozen before data; 20 archived verdicts and 8 closed routes accompany the paper.

</details>


### [252] [OSR: Output Space Redistribution for Adaptive Label Removal in Classification Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03972)
*Minyi Peng,Darian Gunamardi,Ivan Tjuawinata,Yongsen Zheng,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过输出空间统计重分布来近似标签移除后模型置信度的新方法，无需原始数据即可实现高效、隐私保护的分类模型更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标签移除方案（基于重训练或特征空间调整）存在依赖原始数据、计算存储成本高、结果不一致、可扩展性差及模型效用下降等局限。

Method: 利用输出空间的统计重分布来近似重训练后模型的置信向量，作为模块化输出滤波器，仅需现有标签和先验输出置信度，无需特征空间调整或损失函数收敛。

Result: 在多个分类任务中，该方法性能与完整重训练相当，同时在计算效率和隐私保护方面具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了标签移除场景下的可扩展性和隐私问题，为动态分类系统提供了高效、低成本的解决方案。

Abstract: Label removal occurs frequently in classification systems with evolving taxonomies, where categories must be dynamically updated or eliminated. To accommodate such changes, classification models must adapt accordingly. Existing solutions, broadly categorized as retraining-based and feature-space-adjustment-based, share common limitations despite their variations, including reliance on access to original data, substantial computational and storage costs, inconsistent results, poor scalability, and degradation of model utility. To address this, we propose a novel approach that leverages statistical redistribution in the output space to approximate the post-removal confidence vectors of a retrained model. Applicable as a modular output filter, our method bypasses the burden of feature-space adjustments or loss-function convergence, alleviating scalability limitations. Furthermore, by requiring only existing labels and prior output confidences, the method potentially mitigates privacy concerns inherent to data-dependent solutions. Extensive experiments demonstrate competitive performance against full retraining, with improvements in computational efficiency and privacy preservation across several classification tasks.

</details>


### [253] [RobustSeiz: An Open-Source Framework for Benchmarking the Robustness of EEG Seizure Detection Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04007)
*Mohammad Mohammadi,Alireza Zarei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RobustSeiz，一个开源、模型无关的框架，用于在部署前通过模拟临床相关的分布偏移（如环境噪声和对抗性输入）来标准化压力测试和评估癫痫检测器的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有的癫痫检测器在留出数据上表现良好，但在面对现实世界中的采集变异性、伪影和对抗性输入时往往失效。目前缺乏标准化的协议来评估检测器在这些受控临床压力下的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建了RobustSeiz框架，将四个公共头皮EEG数据集（CHB-MIT, TUSZ, Siena, SeizeIT1）标准化为BIDS-EEG格式。该框架包含Dockerized GPU流水线，通过在预定义的超参数网格上扫描环境、噪声和对抗性变换，对受试者独立的检测器进行压力测试，并报告样本级和事件级的多项评估指标（如灵敏度、精确度、F1、误报率、起止时间偏差及蒙特卡洛Dropout预测一致性）。

Result: 通过在TUSZ数据集上对现代癫痫检测器进行完整偏移网格的演示，证明了该框架的有效性。加性高斯白噪声（AWGN）分析具体展示了扰动严重程度如何影响检测质量、发作起始时间定位及预测一致性。

Conclusion: RobustSeiz为评估癫痫检测器在真实临床压力下的鲁棒性提供了共享的基准测试标准，将部署前的评估维度从单纯的清洁数据准确率扩展到了更广泛的现实场景适应性。

Abstract: Despite strong performance on held-out electroencephalography (EEG) data, seizure detectors may fail under real-world acquisition variability, artifacts, and adversarial inputs. We introduce RobustSeiz, an open-source, model-agnostic framework that provides a standardized, reproducible protocol for stress-testing and comparing seizure detectors under controlled, clinically motivated distribution shifts before deployment. We standardize four public scalp-EEG corpora (CHB-MIT, TUSZ, Siena, and SeizeIT1) into BIDS-EEG trees and evaluate subject-independent detectors on held-out splits. Environment, noise, and adversarial transforms are swept over predefined hyperparameter grids. Each run reports sample- and event-level sensitivity, precision, F1, false positives per 24 h, Lead and Lag onset timing, and Monte Carlo dropout predictive agreement. RobustSeiz includes a Dockerized GPU pipeline, experiment registry, and full-evaluation and research-subset modes. We demonstrate the framework with a contemporary seizure detector on TUSZ across the complete implemented shift grid; an AWGN analysis illustrates how perturbation severity changes detection quality, onset timing, and predictive agreement. RobustSeiz provides a shared benchmarking standard for evaluating seizure-detector robustness under realistic clinical stressors, extending pre-deployment assessment beyond clean-data accuracy.

</details>


### [254] [Unlocking Lossless Speedups in LLMs via Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04010)
*Subham Sekhar Sahoo,Lingjie Chen,Khiem Pham,Jonathan Geuter,Chaitanya Dwivedi,Varad Pimpalkhute,Yash Akhauri,Alexander Moreno,Mikhail Yurochkin,Zhenting Wang,Mostafa Elhoushi,Nolan Dey,Shane Bergsma,Joel Hestness,John Thickstun,Eric Xing,Zhengzhong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 Uno 的扩散增强型大语言模型，通过解耦自回归权重和轻量级扩散权重，实现了并行 token 生成，在保持自回归模型质量的同时，显著提升了推理速度（最高 3 倍），且在各项基准测试中优于现有的扩散 LLM 和投机解码方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统大语言模型因自回归结构导致的串行 token 生成速度慢的瓶颈问题，同时避免现有扩散 LLM 牺牲模型质量或投机解码需要额外草稿模型的缺点。

Method: 提出扩散增强型 LLM 架构，将参数解耦为通过标准下一词预测目标训练的自回归权重，以及通过简单扩散蒸馏阶段训练的轻量级扩散权重；引入 $\Psi$-Spec 采样器家族，实现无损加速和固定上下文长度下的推理时扩展，无需单独的草稿模型。

Result: Uno 模型在所有评估的批量大小下吞吐量均优于领先的投机解码方法，相比基础自回归模型实现最高 3 倍加速；8B 参数的 Uno 模型在智能体工具使用、编码和长上下文推理基准上，性能优于 26B 的 DiffusionGemma 及专有模型 Mercury 2。

Conclusion: Uno 模型成功结合了自回归模型的高质量与扩散模型的并行生成优势，提供了一种高效、无需额外草稿模型且可复用现有开源权重的加速方案，显著提升了大语言模型的推理效率。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) owe much of their success to next-token prediction (NTP), but their autoregressive (AR) structure requires slow, sequential token generation. To overcome this bottleneck, we introduce diffusion-augmented LLMs, a new class of models that defines an AR model distribution while using diffusion to draw multiple tokens in parallel from that distribution. We decouple the parameters of these models into two sets: AR weights, trained using the standard NTP objective, and lightweight diffusion weights, trained to generate multiple tokens simultaneously. The diffusion weights are learned through a simple Diffusion Distillation phase that adds negligible overhead to existing LLM training pipelines. We also introduce $Ψ$-Spec, a family of samplers that enables lossless acceleration and inference-time scaling at a fixed context length. Unlike speculative decoding, our method requires no separate draft model. Unlike diffusion LLMs (d-LLMs), it accelerates generation without sacrificing the quality of the underlying AR model. The resulting models, called Uno, can be trained from scratch or built by augmenting existing open-weight AR LLMs. Uno achieves higher throughput than leading speculative-decoding methods at every evaluated batch size and delivers up to $3\times$ speedups over the base AR model, including at the largest batch size supported by the device. Notably, our 8B Uno model outperforms the leading open d-LLM, the 26B DiffusionGemma, and the proprietary Mercury 2 across all evaluated benchmarks in agentic tool use, coding, and long-context reasoning. We release code and checkpoints at: https://s-sahoo.github.io/uno/

</details>


### [255] [A location-invariant estimator of extremal quantile treatment effects for heavy-tailed distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04018)
*Xin Yu,Shuwei Huang,Jicheng Liu,Jielin Tang,Bolin Wang,Yunxiao Zhang,Tian Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的极端分位数处理效应（QTE）估计方法，解决了现有方法在潜在结果分布发生位置偏移时估计量不具不变性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在应用中，当目标分位数远超数据范围时，估计极端分位数处理效应（QTE）具有重要意义。对于重尾潜在结果，现有的极端QTE估计器依赖于外推和因果极值指数（EVI）估计器，但即使总体QTE具有位置不变性，所得估计器在潜在结果分布发生共同位置偏移时也不具有不变性。

Method: 1. 使用逆倾向得分加权，将位置不变的Fraga EVI估计器适应于因果设定。2. 用基于差分的方案替换原始外推公式，使得在取分位数差值时位置参数抵消，从而获得位置不变的QTE估计器。

Result: 建立了所提出的极端QTE估计器的一致性和渐近正态性，并提供了一致的方差估计器，从而实现渐近有效的推断。

Conclusion: 模拟研究证实了所提出方法的位置不变性、对阈值的稳定性以及覆盖率。

Abstract: Quantile treatment effects (QTEs) measure the effect of a treatment on the distribution of an outcome, and their estimation at extreme quantile levels is of central interest in applications where the target quantiles lie far beyond the range of the data. For heavy-tailed potential outcomes, existing extremal QTE estimators rely on extrapolation combined with a causal extreme value index (EVI) estimator, but the resulting estimator is not invariant under a common location shift of the potential outcome distributions, even though the population QTE is. We address this issue in two steps. First, we adapt the location-invariant Fraga estimator of the EVI to the causal setting using inverse propensity score weighting. Second, we replace the original extrapolation formula with a difference-based scheme, under which the location parameter cancels when quantile differences are taken. The resulting QTE estimator is therefore location invariant. We establish the consistency and asymptotic normality of the proposed extremal QTE estimators, and provide a consistent variance estimator, leading to asymptotically valid inference. A simulation study confirms the location invariance, the stability with respect to the threshold, and the coverage of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [256] [Subspace Inference Enables Efficient Active Reward Learning from Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04066)
*Yutai Zhou,Erdem Bıyık*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PreferenceEKF，一种通过扩展卡尔曼滤波在低维参数子空间中进行序贯贝叶斯推断的方法，以解决 RLHF 中奖励模型不确定性量化和样本效率问题，在 D4RL 和 V-D4RL 基准上表现出更好的样本效率、运行时间、可扩展性和校准性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF 虽然强大但样本效率低，主动学习是合成信息性偏好查询的关键，但大神经网络奖励模型的有效不确定性量化仍是主要挑战。

Method: 将主动偏好学习框架化为序贯贝叶斯滤波问题，使用扩展卡尔曼滤波在低维参数子空间中进行序贯推断，持续更新奖励模型后验，实现神经网络参数的可扩展采样以高效计算主动奖励学习的采集函数。

Result: 在 D4RL 和 V-D4RL 基准上的实验表明，该方法在样本效率、运行时间、可扩展性和校准性方面优于其他贝叶斯深度学习方法，学习到的奖励模型能带来具有竞争力的离线强化学习策略性能。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了可扩展贝叶斯方法在 RLHF 中基于偏好的奖励建模的潜力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has emerged as a powerful yet sample-inefficient approach for learning reward models from human preferences, making active learning a critical component in synthesizing informative preference queries. However, effective uncertainty quantification required for active learning remains a key challenge for large neural network reward models. In this paper, we introduce PreferenceEKF, a sample-efficient approach that tracks reward model uncertainty by framing active preference learning as a sequential Bayesian filtering problem. Instead of relying on computationally prohibitive posterior inference over the full neural network parameter space, our method performs sequential inference via an extended Kalman filter within a low-dimensional parameter subspace, continuously updating the reward model posterior as new preference queries arrive. Our approach enables scalable sampling of neural network parameters to efficiently compute acquisition functions for active reward learning. Experiments on the D4RL and V-D4RL benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves better sample efficiency, runtime, scalability, and calibration compared to other Bayesian deep learning approaches, and the learned reward models lead to competitive offline reinforcement learning policy performance. This highlights the potential of scalable Bayesian methods for preference-based reward modeling in RLHF. Our code is available at https://github.com/yutaizhou/bnn_pref.

</details>


### [257] [Conditioning Degenerate Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04090)
*Uğur Aydın,Tamer Başar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用因果最优传输（Causal Optimal Transport）为扩散过程定义近似损失函数，以在底层密度不存在或不光滑的极端假设下，识别用于引导的最小熵控制，从而摆脱对传统得分函数（score functions）的过度依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前条件生成模型严重依赖得分函数进行引导的问题，特别是当扩散过程具有奇异扩散系数，且底层（条件）密度不存在或不光滑时，传统方法失效的挑战。

Method: 采用因果最优传输理论，利用条件扩散过程的可料表示性质（predictable representation property）及其相关鞅问题（martingale problem）的良好定性（well-posedness，参考Üstünel的工作），来定义近似损失函数。

Result: 在最小化假设条件下，成功构建了能够识别最小熵控制（minimum-entropy control）的近似损失函数，实现了无需平滑密度假设下的有效引导。

Conclusion: 该方法通过因果最优传输框架，为奇异扩散系数和非平滑密度场景下的生成模型引导提供了严谨的数学基础和新的解决方案。

Abstract: Current conditioned generative models heavily rely on score functions for guidance during training. When the generative model is a diffusion process with a singular diffusion coefficient and the underlying (conditional) densities either do not exist or are not smooth, we use causal optimal transport to define \emph{approximate} loss functions that identify a minimum-entropy control for guidance under minimal assumptions. Our approach relies on causal optimal transport and its characterization through the predictable representation property of (conditioned) diffusion processes whose associated martingale problem is well posed, à la Üstünel.

</details>


### [258] [Hardware-Aware FP4 FlashAttention-4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04105)
*Robert Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Blackwell的4-bit浮点张量核心在注意力机制中因softmax转换和片上依赖导致加速受限的问题，提出Direct-P非因果推理方法和因果路径优化，实现显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blackwell架构的FP4张量核心在处理注意力机制时，由于softmax转换和片上依赖问题，当矩阵乘积缩小时无法自动提升速度，需要优化量化路径以充分发挥硬件性能。

Method: 提出Direct-P方法将分数直接映射到FP4概率用于非因果推理；设计因果路径通过保存的量化查询和键重建概率，并使用FP8梯度操作数；分布式训练保留FP8概率和值以避免训练发散。

Result: 在NVIDIA GB200上，Direct-P实现最高2.13倍BF16前向吞吐加速；因果路径使80亿参数单GPU更新加速1.14倍；MXFP4概率/值训练轨迹均发散，需保留FP8。

Conclusion: 通过Direct-P和因果路径优化，可有效利用FP4张量核心提升注意力机制效率，但分布式训练需保留FP8精度以保证收敛性。

Abstract: Blackwell's 4-bit floating-point (FP4) tensor cores do not automatically make attention faster because softmax conversion and on-chip dependencies dominate once its matrix products shrink. We address this with \emph{Direct-P} for noncausal inference and a causal path that passes the forward quantization directly into backward. Direct-P maps scores directly to FP4 probabilities and reaches up to 2.13$\times$ the bfloat16 (BF16) forward throughput on an NVIDIA GB200. The causal path reconstructs probabilities from saved quantized queries and keys and uses 8-bit floating-point (FP8) gradient operands, accelerating a complete single-GPU 8-billion-parameter update by up to 1.14$\times$. Matched distributed training retains FP8 probabilities and values; every tested MXFP4 probability/value training trajectory diverges.

</details>


### [259] [Constant regret in general games via higher-order optimism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04113)
*Omar Abbadi,Rida Laraki,Panayotis Mertikopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HOOD的解耦学习算法，通过在提升的策略空间上结合折扣高阶预测器与熵正则化，有效抑制了博弈过程中的剧烈振荡，从而在任意N人标准型博弈中实现了$O(N^3\log^2 K)$的个体遗憾界，显著优于同期工作的$O(N^{21}\log^{4} K)$结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在一般博弈中实现常数遗憾（constant regret）的主要障碍，即先前尝试中由诱导博弈序列产生的大幅振荡问题。

Method: 提出高阶乐观折扣算法（HOOD），这是乐观跟随正则化领导者（OptFTRL）的一种变体。该方法结合了折扣的$(N+1)$阶预测器，并在博弈策略空间的适当“提升”上应用熵正则化，以受控方式阻尼振荡。

Result: 在任意具有最多$K$个动作的$N$人标准型博弈中，该算法保证了$O(N^3\log^2 K)$的个体遗憾，且该界限在整个博弈时间范围内一致成立。

Conclusion: HOOD算法通过特定的技术组合成功克服了前人未能解决的振荡难题，实现了比同期独立工作（Liu等人，$O(N^{21}\log^{4} K)$）更优的遗憾界限，为一般博弈中的低遗憾学习提供了新途径。

Abstract: We introduce an uncoupled learning algorithm which, when employed by all players of an arbitrary $N$-player normal form game with up to $K$ actions per player, guarantees $O(N^3\log^2 K)$ individual regret, uniformly over the horizon of play. The proposed algorithm - which we call higher-order optimism with discounting (HOOD) is a variant of optimistic follow-the-regularized-leader (OptFTRL) that combines a discounted $(N+1)$-th order predictor with entropic regularization over a suitable "lifting" of the game's strategy space. This combination of ingredients is purposefully designed to dampen large oscillations of the induced sequence of play in a controlled manner, removing in this way a key stumbling block of previous attempts to achieve constant regret in general games. Our approach bears several striking similarities to the concurrent - and completely independent - work of Liu, Farina, and Ozdaglar (arXiv:2608.31166), who very recently derived an $O(N^{21}\log^{4} K)$ regret bound through the use of higher-order optimism and an exponential moving average estimator.

</details>


### [260] [Prospective Coding Improves Learning in Deep Continuous-Time Recurrent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04134)
*Shivang Rawat,Mirko Morello,Flaviano Morone,David J. Heeger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出递归正交滤波器（RQFs）和前瞻性输入编码，解决深度连续时间循环网络中信号延迟和梯度衰减问题，在语音和长序列任务上实现高效性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度连续时间循环网络中，时间积分虽赋予网络记忆能力，但会导致底层信号延迟和顶层误差衰减，影响深层网络训练效果。

Method: 从能量模型推导RQF动力学，设计无参数双抽头更新使每层底层输入具有前瞻性，并将此校正扩展至一般对角状态空间模型（SSMs）。

Result: 在RQFs、S5和ORGaNICs模型上验证，前瞻性变体在全BPTT和空间反向传播下均优于或等于非前瞻性对照；31.9k参数的6层RQF在语音命令任务达96.09%准确率，64宽度6层RQF在Path-X任务达83.56%准确率。

Conclusion: RQFs是参数高效的循环基底，前瞻性输入编码是深度连续时间循环网络的有效输入侧校正方法。

Abstract: Temporal integration gives continuous-time recurrent networks memory, but in deep stacks it also delays bottom-up signals and attenuates top-down errors. We develop Recursive Quadrature Filters (RQFs), biologically motivated complex-valued temporal filters that are a special case of diagonal state-space models (SSMs), and ask whether this failure mode can be addressed by making each layer's bottom-up input prospective. Starting from an energy model, we derive the RQF dynamics and show that each RQF is a band-pass filter whose learnable parameters control its tuning frequency and bandwidth. We then make each layer's bottom-up input prospective using a parameter-free two-tap update that leaves the recurrent transition and parallel scan unchanged. We extend this correction to general diagonal SSMs and show that it mitigates depth-dependent gradient attenuation when temporal gradients are truncated, i.e., spatial-only backpropagation. We evaluate the intervention in RQFs, S5, and ORGaNICs (a nonlinear gated RNN) trained using full backpropagation through time (BPTT) and spatial-only backpropagation. Under full BPTT, prospective variants match or outperform their non-prospective controls in every model and configuration. A non-residual width-32 six-layer RQF reaches 96.09% accuracy on raw-audio Speech Commands with 31.9k parameters; a width-64 six-layer RQF reaches 83.56% on the 16,384-step Path-X task. These results identify RQFs as a parameter-efficient recurrent substrate and prospective-input coding as an input-side correction for deep continuous-time recurrent networks.

</details>


### [261] [A Low-Cost, Open Platform for End-to-End Autonomous Driving on a Miniature Ackermann Vehicle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04147)
*Gustavo Claudio Karl Couto,Eric Aislan Antonelo,Gabriel George Zipperer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种低成本开源的端到端自动驾驶实验平台，结合微型阿克曼车辆、实体赛道与Webots数字孪生，验证了基于命令条件的行为克隆策略，并证明了利用数字孪生生成合成数据结合模拟到真实（sim-to-real）图像转换技术，能显著提升策略在复杂路径下的闭环执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自动驾驶研究中仿真方法与现实执行之间的鸿沟，提供一个可控、低成本且可复现的实验环境，以支持模拟到真实（sim-to-real）迁移学习的研究。

Method: 构建包含实体车辆、打印赛道、数据采集工具及Webots数字孪生的混合平台；实施命令条件的行为克隆（behavior cloning），输入为车载摄像头图像和高层导航指令，输出为转向和速度；利用数字孪生生成合成数据，并采用学习到的模拟到真实图像翻译器缩小外观差异，训练高容量策略。

Result: 实体实验中，策略平均横向误差为6.1厘米，接近人类演示的4.7厘米；数字孪生中，视场角从58度增至120度使误差从35.6厘米降至3.3厘米；仅“合成数据+真实演示”训练的高容量策略能完成全部四条赛道闭环，而基线模型或仅用真实数据训练的模型完成度较低。

Conclusion: 该平台是进行模拟到真实自动驾驶研究的有效测试床，所建立的命令条件模仿学习基线具有实用价值，开源发布将促进该领域的可复现研究。

Abstract: This paper presents a low-cost, open experimental platform for research in end-to-end autonomous driving with miniature Ackermann vehicles. The platform combines a physical vehicle, a printed urban track, data collection tools, trajectory registration, and a Webots digital twin, enabling controlled experiments that connect simulation-based autonomous-driving methods to real-world execution. As a first baseline, we implement command-conditioned behavior cloning, in which a neural policy receives an on-board camera image and a high-level navigation command and outputs steering and speed. The system is evaluated both on the physical vehicle and in simulation. In real closed-loop experiments, the learned policy follows lanes and executes commanded turns, reaching a mean cross-track error of 6.1 cm with respect to the reference route, close to the 4.7 cm observed in human demonstrations. In the digital twin, camera field of view has a strong effect on performance, reducing the mean cross-track error from 35.6 to 3.3 cm when widened from 58 to 120 degrees. Using the digital twin to generate synthetic driving data and a learned sim-to-real image translator to reduce the appearance gap, we further show that a higher-capacity policy trained on this synthetic data combined with real demonstrations is the only configuration that completes all four track routes in closed loop, whereas the compact baseline and the same network trained on real data alone complete fewer. These results establish the open platform as a practical testbed for sim-to-real studies and provide an initial command-conditioned imitation-learning baseline; we release it to support reproducible research.

</details>


### [262] [Robust PAC Learning of Concurrent Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04189)
*Angel Y. He,David Parker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对具有转移不确定性的广义和并发随机博弈（CSG）的PAC学习框架，解决了纳什均衡（NE）存在性挑战，并提供了多项式样本复杂度的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决广义和并发随机博弈中转移不确定性下的PAC学习问题，同时处理纳什均衡存在性这一核心挑战，现有框架缺乏对均衡存在性的理论保证。

Method: 维护数据驱动的L1置信集，求解鲁棒CSG以计算社会福利最优的ε-NE，采用基于鲁棒MDP的探索机制驱动联合状态 - 动作覆盖，引入纳什边际表征来推理均衡存在性。

Result: 在最小可达性条件下，算法在多项式数量的轨迹样本后终止，样本复杂度为Õ(R_max^2 H^4 |S|^2 |A| / (p_reach ε^2))，基准测试显示近最优性能且与理论一致。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效处理均衡存在性问题，提供ε-近似NE或无精确NE存在的证明，具有理论保证和良好的实证表现。

Abstract: We introduce the first Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) learning framework for general-sum concurrent stochastic games (CSGs) with transition uncertainty, while addressing the challenge of Nash equilibrium (NE) existence. Our algorithm maintains data-driven $L^1$ confidence sets over transition kernels and solves a robust CSG to compute a social-welfare optimal $\varepsilon$-NE, using a robust MDP-based exploration mechanism to drive joint state-action coverage. Crucially, we introduce a Nash margin characterisation that enables principled reasoning about equilibrium existence: the framework either returns an $\varepsilon$-approximate NE whose social-welfare value is $\varepsilon$-close to optimal, or provides a sound certificate that no exact NE exists. Under a minimum reachability condition $p_{\mathrm{reach}}>0$ over relevant state-action pairs, the algorithm terminates after a polynomial number of trajectory samples, with sample complexity $\widetilde{O}\left( {R_{\max}^2 H^4 |S|^2 |A| / (p_{\mathrm{reach}} \varepsilon^2)} \right)$. Empirical results on benchmark CSGs demonstrate near-optimal performance, correct handling of equilibrium (non-)existence, and sample complexity consistent with theory.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [263] [Occupancy-based Quantile Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03104)
*Zihao Shi,Huajun Xi,Bingyi Jing,Hongxin Wei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于占用率的量化风险控制（OQRC）方法，通过将损失空间划分为有序校准损失区间并估计测试损失分布，解决了现有量化风险控制方法过于保守或缺乏有限样本保证的问题，实现了紧致的风险控制边界和$\mathcal{O}_p(n^{-1/2})$的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量化风险控制扩展虽然适应了更广泛的风险概念，但存在两个主要局限：一是方法过于保守，二是缺乏严格的有限样本保证。为了在安全部署机器学习模型时获得更紧致且有效的风险控制边界，需要一种新的解决方案。

Method: 提出基于占用率的量化风险控制（OQRC）。核心思想是将风险控制表述为有限占用问题：利用有序校准损失对损失空间进行划分（分箱），估计测试损失在这些区间内的分布，并通过每个区间内达到的最大损失来上界化风险。随后，选择参数$\lambda$，使得该上界以高概率$1-\delta$不超过预设阈值$\alpha$。

Result: 理论分析证明了OQRC具有有限样本保证，其风险控制边界紧致，并以$\mathcal{O}_p(n^{-1/2})$的可证明速率收敛到最优边界。实验结果显示，在常见基准测试上，该方法将风险差距（risk gap）降低了高达78.64%。

Conclusion: OQRC是一种新颖且有效的方法，成功克服了现有量化风险控制方法的保守性和理论缺陷，为机器学习模型的安全部署提供了具有有限样本有效性且紧致的风险控制框架。

Abstract: Conformal risk control is an emerging framework for the safe deployment of machine learning models with finite-sample guarantees. To accommodate a broader class of risk notions, quantile risk control extends this framework to quantile-based risk measures. However, existing methods either suffer from excessive conservatism or lack rigorous finite-sample guarantees. To address these limitations, we introduce Occupancy-based Quantile Risk Control (OQRC), a novel method that provides tight risk control bounds with finite-sample validity. Our key idea is to formulate risk control as a finite-occupancy problem by partitioning the loss space with the ordered calibration losses. Specifically, we estimate the distribution of test losses across the resulting bins and upper-bound the risk by the maximum loss attained within each bin. We then select the parameter $λ$ such that this upper bound does not exceed a predefined threshold $α$ with high probability $1-δ$. Theoretically, we establish a finite-sample guarantee showing that OQRC yields tight risk control bounds that converge to the optimal bounds at a provable rate of $\mathcal{O}_ p(n^{-1/2})$. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, reducing the risk gap by up to 78.64\% on common benchmarks.

</details>


### [264] [A Closed-Form Formula for Consistent Lipschitz Regression on Metric Spaces with Sparse Neural Network Realizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03129)
*Ruiyang Hong,Hrad Ghoukasian,Anastasis Kratsios*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简单的闭式“两阶段”组合公式，用于在度量空间上从噪声观测中重构未知 Lipschitz 函数，实现了零优化误差的高概率一致恢复保证，并证明了其在函数空间、参数空间和前向传播三个维度上的最优性，同时给出了具体的 ReLU-MLP 和 Transformer 实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决经典机器学习方法（如 KRR 和 SVR）具有闭式解或凸优化目标从而在计算和分析上易于处理，而深度神经网络通常缺乏这些特性的问题，旨在为深度学习提供一种兼具解析可处理性和理论保证的替代方案。

Method: 引入一种闭式“两阶段”组合公式 $\hat{f}$，用于在度量空间 $(\mathcal X, \rho)$ 上从 $N$ 个独立同分布的噪声观测中重构未知 Lipschitz 函数 $f$，该方法不依赖对近似经验风险最小化（ERM）的预言机访问，并导出了深度为 $\mathcal{O}(\log(N))$、非零参数为 $\mathcal{O}(N)$ 的 ReLU-MLP 和多头 Transformer 具体实现。

Result: 主要结果包括：1) 获得了同时控制逼近误差和统计误差的高概率一致（$L^{\infty}$）恢复保证，且优化误差为零；2) 证明了公式的三重最优性：在 Ahlfors 正则度量空间上达到最优 fat-shattering 维数；参数依赖性具有最大数值稳定性；输入依赖性具有最大正则性（匹配目标函数 Lipschitz 常数）。

Conclusion: 所提出的闭式公式不仅在理论上提供了严格的恢复保证和最优性证明，还展示了其在实际深度学习架构（如 ReLU-MLP 和 Transformer）中的高效可实现性，为连接经典统计学习理论与现代深度学习提供了新的解析工具。

Abstract: Several classical machine-learning methods, such as KRRs and SVRs, are both computationally and analytically tractable since their estimators either admit closed-form expressions or are obtained by minimizing convex training objectives; neither feature is generally available for deep neural networks. We address this by introducing a simple closed-form ``two-stage'' compositional formula $\hat{f}$ for reconstructing an unknown Lipschitz function $f:\mathcal{X}\to \mathbb{R}$ on a metric space $(\mathcal X,ρ)$ from $N$ i.i.d. noisy observations.
  Our main result is a high-probability uniform ($L^{\infty}$) recovery guarantee that jointly controls approximation and statistical errors while enjoying an optimization error of zero; in particular, we do not assume oracle access to an approximate ERM. Our secondary main results establish the optimality of our formula in three complementary senses. 1) Function space: On Ahlfors-regular metric spaces, the hypothesis class parameterized by our formula attains the optimal fat-shattering dimension. 2) Parameter space: Its dependence on the parameters is maximally numerically stable, in the sense that a smaller approximation error cannot be achieved with a smaller Lipschitz dependence on the model parameters. 3) Forward pass: Its dependence on the input is maximally regular, matching the Lipschitz constant of the target function $f$. When $\mathcal X=[0,1]^d$ is equipped with the $\ell^\infty$ norm, $\hat{f}$ admits algorithmic ReLU-MLP and exact ReLU-multi-head transformer realizations of depth $\mathcal{O}(\log(N))$ with $\mathcal{O}(N)$ nonzero parameters.

</details>


### [265] [ALRA: Adaptive Local Relational Alignment for Logit-Based Pre-training Distillation of Autoregressive Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03355)
*Quang Hoang Trung,Quang Huu Hieu,Nguyen Van Hoang Phuc,Vo Nguyen Le Duy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出ALRA（自适应局部关系对齐）方法，通过结合学生提议与教师指导，改进自回归语言模型的对数几率知识蒸馏，在9个零样本基准测试中显著提升200M和500M参数学生模型的平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于logit的知识蒸馏方法通常在整个词汇表上对齐教师和学生模型的下一词分布，但这种全局目标忽略了可能token替代方案之间的相对偏好；而现有的局部方法要么仅从教师模型选择候选token（可能遗漏学生认为可能的token），要么仅从学生模型选择（训练早期排名不准确）。

Method: 提出ALRA框架，在每个有效预测位置，由学生提议可能的token，同时包含教师最可能的token作为锚点；根据教师在该候选集内相对于当前批次的概率分布广度调整选中token数量；采用自适应局部分散保留质量匹配项，并分别匹配选中和剩余词汇区域的相对token分布；使用学生加权成对关系对齐，强调高概率token对且学生概率差距小的对。

Result: 在The Pile数据集上对随机初始化的200M和500M参数学生模型进行实验，在9个零样本基准测试中分别获得36.62%和37.40%的平均准确率；ALRA超过最强竞争蒸馏基线0.94和0.83个百分点，相比无蒸馏预训练分别提升2.31和2.91个百分点。

Conclusion: ALRA方法通过自适应局部关系对齐有效改进了自回归语言模型的知识蒸馏，在多个基准测试中证明了其优越性，特别是在处理token相对偏好和候选选择方面表现出色。

Abstract: Logit-based knowledge distillation for autoregressive language models usually aligns teacher and student next-token distributions over the entire vocabulary. However, this global objective overlooks relative preferences among likely token alternatives. Existing local approaches often select candidate tokens from either the teacher or the student alone. Teacher-only selection can miss tokens that the student considers likely, while student-only selection can rely on an inaccurate ranking early in training. We propose Adaptive Local Relational Alignment (ALRA), a position-specific framework combining student proposals with teacher guidance. At each valid prediction position, the student proposes likely tokens, while the teacher's most probable token is included as an anchor. ALRA adjusts the number of selected tokens according to how broadly the teacher distributes probability within this candidate set relative to the current batch. Adaptive Local Divergence retains the mass-matching term and separately matches the relative token distributions within the selected and remaining vocabulary regions. Unlike the exact full-vocabulary decomposition, it replaces the teacher-mass coefficients of the two conditional terms with unit coefficients, preventing either term from being downweighted solely because its region has low teacher probability. Student-Weighted Pairwise Relational Alignment emphasizes high-probability token pairs with small student probability gaps and gives less weight to unlikely or clearly separated pairs. Experiments on The Pile with randomly initialized 200M- and 500M-parameter students across nine zero-shot benchmarks yield average accuracies of 36.62% and 37.40%. ALRA exceeds the strongest competing distillation baseline by 0.94 and 0.83 percentage points and improves over pre-training without distillation by 2.31 and 2.91 points, respectively.

</details>


### [266] [Towards a Statistical Understanding of Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03501)
*Siyuan He,Bokai Yang,Jie Hu,Ziwen Gao,Yuhong Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从统计学习角度分析混合专家模型（MoE），揭示路由、稀疏激活和共享专家如何通过局部聚合机制影响模型的近似-估计-计算权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MoE架构在现代大规模模型中日益重要，但其设计选择（尤其是路由、稀疏激活和共享专家）的统计作用仍未被充分理解，现有理论多局限于参数化或正确设定的MoE模型。

Method: 将MoE视为一种局部聚合形式，推导密集和稀疏路由的Oracle风险界，分离近似误差、专家学习误差和路由估计误差，并通过输入空间几何解释门控机制，分析共享专家的作用。

Result: 证明了稀疏Top-K路由能在保持局部聚合优势的同时控制每输入计算量；共享专家可提取共同预测结构，使路由专家专注于残差局部变化；建立了基于输入依赖专家聚合的统一统计框架。

Conclusion: MoE中的专家专业化和计算权衡由局部预测结构决定，该研究为理解MoE架构提供了统一的统计理论框架。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures increase model capacity by combining a collection of expert predictors through input-dependent routing, while often activating only a small subset of experts for each input. Despite their growing importance in modern large-scale models, the statistical roles of their design choices, especially routing, sparse activation, and shared experts, remain only partially understood, as existing theory has largely focused on parametric or correctly specified MoE models. In this paper, we view MoE as a form of localized aggregation and show how this localization reshapes the approximation-estimation-computation tradeoff. We derive oracle risk bounds for learning dense and sparse routing with evolving experts, separating approximation, expert-learning, and router-estimation errors, and characterize how sparse Top-K routing can retain the benefits of localized aggregation while controlling per-input computation. We also interpret gating through the geometry of input space, relating routing performance to regions of local expert advantage, and show how shared experts, as adopted in architectures such as DeepSeekMoE, can extract common predictive structure so that routed experts focus on residual local variation. Together, these results provide a unified statistical framework for understanding MoE through input-dependent expert aggregation, in which expert specialization and computational tradeoffs are governed by local predictive structure.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [267] [Direct Satellite-to-Device Communications: From Cooperative Task Offloading to Non-Cooperative Access Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02955)
*Sai Huang,Wanli Ni,Ke Lv,Pengcheng Zhang,Yurui Zheng,Menghan Zhang,Zihui Gong,Zhiyong Feng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向6G非地面网络的多功能直连卫星到设备（DS2D）系统，通过D3QN算法优化协作场景下的任务卸载，并利用Transformer模型实现非协作场景下的盲信号检测与自动调制分类，显著降低了延迟并提升了低信噪比下的检测与分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决DS2D通信在实际部署中面临的双重挑战：一是协作场景下动态卫星信道条件（如多普勒频移、快衰落）及受限的计算资源问题；二是非协作场景下未授权接入引发的频谱安全威胁。

Method: 提出一种支持协作任务卸载与非协作接入监控的DS2D系统架构。在协作通信中，集成信道估计模块与决斗双深度Q网络（D3QN）以动态优化任务卸载策略；在非协作通信中，采用基于Transformer的模型进行盲信号检测和自动调制分类（AMC）。

Result: 仿真结果显示：1）D3QN算法相比静态关联策略将平均延迟降低了225%；2）信号检测模型对DS2D信号的平均存在检测概率达到90.5%；3）AMC算法在不同信噪比下表现优异，在低信噪比区域准确率较现有方法提升9.4%。

Conclusion: 所提出的DS2D系统能有效应对动态信道与安全接入挑战，通过深度强化学习与Transformer架构的结合，显著提升了6G非地面网络在远程及欠发达地区的连接性与边缘计算能力。

Abstract: Direct satellite-to-device (DS2D) communication is emerging as a transformative paradigm for extending ubiquitous connectivity and edge computing capabilities to remote and underserved regions within 6G non-terrestrial networks. However, practical deployment faces dual critical challenges: i) dynamic satellite channel conditions (e.g., severe Doppler shifts, fast fading) and constrained satellite computing resources in cooperative scenarios; and ii) unauthorized satellite access introduces significant spectrum security threats in non-cooperative scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose a versatile DS2D system that supports cooperative task offloading and non-cooperative access monitoring. For cooperative DS2D communications, we integrate a channel estimation module with a dueling double deep Q-network (D3QN) to dynamically optimize task offloading strategy. For non-cooperative DS2D communications, we propose Transformer-based models to enable blind signal detection and automatic modulation classification (AMC). Simulation results show that: 1) The D3QN algorithm reduces average latency by up to 225\% compared to static association policies. 2) Our signal detection model achieves an average presence detection probability of 90.5\% for DS2D signals. 3) The proposed AMC algorithm achieves superior performance across different signal-to-noise ratios (SNRs), with a 9.4\% accuracy gain in low-SNR regimes compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [268] [Adaptive Beam Hopping and Power Control for Dual-Layer Over-the-Air Online Federated Learning in LEO Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03202)
*Zhendong Li,Shaojie Wang,Zhou Su,Zihao Zhang,Haixia Peng,Nan Cheng,Ying Wang,Wen Chen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了低轨卫星网络中支持空中计算的在线联邦学习，提出了一种基于深度强化学习（PPO）的双层空中聚合架构，联合优化波束跳跃和功率控制，以最大化长期数据利用率并满足聚合精度要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低轨卫星网络中，设备持续收集新数据，未训练样本会随时间失去新鲜度，同时卫星拓扑时变、数据生成动态，且存在波束预算、发射功率限制和全局均方误差约束，导致传统优化方法难以处理这一NP难的混合整数非线性规划问题。

Method: 将问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程，开发基于近端策略优化（PPO）的深度强化学习框架，联合优化自适应波束跳跃和功率控制，采用MSE感知奖励函数平衡数据利用率与聚合精度。

Result: 数值仿真结果表明，所提算法在满足均方误差要求的前提下，长期数据利用率和联邦学习收敛速度均优于其他基准方案。

Conclusion: 基于PPO的深度强化学习框架能有效解决低轨卫星网络中空中计算赋能的在线联邦学习资源调度问题，实现高效数据利用与高精度聚合的平衡。

Abstract: This paper investigates over-the-air (OTA) computation enabled online federated learning (FL) in low-Earth orbit (LEO) satellite networks. Specifically, we consider a dual-layer OTA aggregation architecture, where ground devices upload analog model updates to serving satellites via uplink OTA aggregation, and satellites forward the aggregated signals to a data processing center through the second round OTA aggregation. Then, we formulate a long-term data-utilization maximization problem in which devices continuously collect new data and untrained samples gradually lose freshness. The problem is subject to the satellite beam budget, transmit-power limit, and global mean squared error (MSE) constraint that governs end-to-end aggregation distortion. This yields a coupled mixed-integer nonlinear programming (MINLP) problem, involving tightly coupled discrete beam-hopping decisions and continuous power control. Due to the combinatorial action space and nonconvex constraints, the problem is NP-hard and computationally intractable. Furthermore, the time-varying satellite topology and dynamic data generation render it a sequential decision-making problem, necessitating adaptive online scheduling. To address these issues, we cast the problem as a Markov decision process and develop a proximal policy optimization (PPO)-based deep reinforcement learning framework that jointly optimizes adaptive beam hopping and power control, using an MSE-aware reward to balance data utilization and aggregation accuracy. Numerical simulation results verify that the proposed algorithm consistently outperforms other benchmark schemes, achieving superior long-term data utilization and faster FL convergence while satisfying the MSE requirement.

</details>


### [269] [Variational Probabilistic Quantization for Secret Key Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03205)
*Xinyang Li,Vlad C. Andrei,Peter J. Gu,Yiqi Chen,Ullrich J. Mönich,Holger Boche*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出变分概率量化（VPQ）方法，通过神经编码器直接将相关源映射为离散密钥，利用变分对抗目标优化密钥率，并结合线性码偏移安全草图实现密钥协调，无需单独的隐私放大步骤，在性能上优于现有基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统物理层安全密钥生成流程中人工量化、后处理隐私放大以及缺乏与密钥率直接挂钩的优化目标等局限性。

Method: 提出变分概率量化（VPQ），利用神经编码器将相关观测直接映射到离散密钥字母表，通过包含熵、不匹配和泄露项的变分对抗目标进行训练，并采用线性码偏移安全草图进行密钥协调。

Result: 证明了VPQ损失函数是诱导源单向秘密密钥容量的下界，推导了给定字母表大小和不匹配概率下的最优最坏情况密钥率闭式解；在高斯衰落信道实验中，VPQ相比经典及最新学习型基线泄露更少，且Reed-Solomon协调方案在预测的有限块长差距内运行。

Conclusion: VPQ框架通过端到端优化直接最大化密钥率，理论上保证了容量下界，并在实际信道条件下展现出比现有方法更低的信息泄露和高效的密钥协调性能。

Abstract: Secret key generation from correlated observations at Alice and Bob, in the presence of an eavesdropper Eve, underpins physical-layer security. Classical pipelines quantize by hand, amplify privacy afterwards, and optimize no objective tied to a key rate. We propose Variational Probabilistic Quantization (VPQ): neural encoders that map the correlated sources directly into a discrete key alphabet, trained by a variational adversarial objective whose entropy, mismatch, and leakage terms match the three terms of the one-way secret key rate. A linear code-offset secure sketch then reconciles the encoder outputs into an identical key without a separate privacy amplification step. We prove that the VPQ losses lower-bound the one-way secret key capacity of the induced source, and derive in closed form the optimal worst-case key rate over the source class of a given alphabet size and mismatch probability, attained by finite-field linear sketches. On Gaussian fading channels, VPQ leaks less to a correlated eavesdropper than one classical and two recent learning-based baselines, and Reed--Solomon reconciliation operates within the predicted finite-blocklength gap.

</details>


### [270] [A Joint Power-Privacy Control Framework for Decentralized Learning over Heterogeneous Wireless Multicasting Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03245)
*Amir Ziaeddini,Yauhen Yakimenka,Joerg Kliewer*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合功率控制和隐私保护的分散式学习框架，通过功率分配策略在无线多播网络中实现模型训练与隐私泄露的平衡，并在CIFAR-10数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无线多播网络中进行分散式学习时，需要同时考虑通信约束（如非对称信道增益、异构发射功率）和隐私保护需求，现有方法难以在保证隐私的同时优化学习性能。

Method: 采用行随机邻接矩阵表示通信网络，通过显式的功率分配策略将节点的最大发射功率在模型系数和高斯噪声之间进行分配，以实现差分隐私保护，并控制每轮迭代的最大隐私泄露水平。

Result: 证明了算法具有O(logT)的累积后悔界，在CIFAR-10数据集上的实验表明，在不同隐私级别、客户端数量和图拓扑结构下均表现出强性能，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的算法在现实无线通信约束下能有效平衡学习性能与隐私保护，为分散式学习在无线网络中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a decentralized learning framework that incorporates both power control and privacy guarantees. Specifically, we enable a set of clients in a wireless multicast network to jointly train a common model while maintaining a prescribed per-iteration maximum privacy leakage level. The communication network is represented by a rowstochastic adjacency matrix, allowing us to capture asymmetric channel gains as well as heterogeneous maximum transmit power levels. Differential privacy is enforced through an explicit powersplitting strategy that allocates each node's limited maximum transmit power between model coefficients and injected Gaussian noise, thereby jointly controlling learning performance and privacy leakage. We further prove that the proposed algorithm achieves a cumulative regret bound of O(logT), whereTdenotes the time horizon. To evaluate the practical performance of our approach, we perform comprehensive experiments on the CIFAR-10 dataset under both IID and non-IID data distributions, considering different privacy levels, diverse numbers of clients, and various graph topologies. The results demonstrate strong performance across the considered settings and improved performance over existing methods, highlighting the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm under realistic wireless communication constraints.

</details>


### [271] [Iterative Semantic Decoding for Short Block Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03256)
*Jiafu Hao,Chentao Yue,Mingcheng Nie,Branka Vucetic,Yonghui Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种迭代增强的语义接收机，用于在噪声无线信道上传输自然语言文本。该接收机通过结合信道解码器和语言模型，在SNR超过1.0 dB时实现了比传统短块编码约1.5 dB的BLER增益，同时保持了高于99%的BLEU和ROUGE分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自然语言文本在噪声无线信道传输中的可靠性问题，利用语义信息提升解码性能，克服传统短块编码在低信噪比下的性能局限。

Method: 发送端：字符级交织器对句子进行置换，分段后使用短块码独立编码。接收端：迭代解码器由信道解码器和语言模型组成，中间通过解交织器分散段内突发解码错误。每次迭代中，语言模型对信道解码输出去噪，验证与信道观测一致的字符作为语义信息反馈给信道解码器进行下一轮迭代。

Result: 在Stanford自然语言推理（SNLI）语料库和加性高斯白噪声（AWGN）信道上的仿真显示，该接收机相比传统短块编码获得约1.5 dB的块错误率（BLER）增益，在SNR超过1.0 dB时BLEU和ROUGE分数保持在99%以上。

Conclusion: 所提出的迭代增强语义接收机有效利用了语言模型的语义信息，显著提升了噪声信道下自然语言文本传输的可靠性和质量，为语义通信系统提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes an iteratively enhanced semantic receiver for natural-language text transmission over noisy wireless channels using multiple short block codes. At the transmitter, each sentence is permuted by a character-level interleaver, partitioned into segments, and independently encoded by short block codes. At the receiver, we develop an iterative decoder consisting of a channel decoder and a language model, where a de-interleaver between them disperses the burst decoding errors within each segment across the sentence. In each iteration, the language model denoises the channel decoding output, and the denoised characters verified to be consistent with the channel observations are fed back to the channel decoder as semantic information for the next iteration. Simulation results on the Stanford Natural Language Inference (SNLI) corpus over the additive white Gaussian noise (AWGN) channel show that the proposed receiver achieves approximately 1.5 dB block error rate (BLER) gain over conventional short-block coding, while maintaining BLEU and ROUGE scores above 99% at SNRs beyond 1.0 dB.

</details>


### [272] [Finite-SNR Closed Form for the Square Marčenko-Pastur MIMO Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03356)
*Mohamed Akrout,Robert W. Heath*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对具有全信道状态信息的正方形MIMO信道（收发天线数相等），提出了一种经典的三角参数化方法，解决了以往仅能通过数值方法求解注水截止点的问题，实现了容量与信噪比关于单一角度参数的显式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在正方形MIMO信道（收发天线比为1:1）中，Marčenko-Pastur律的支撑集触及原点，导致注水截止点在有限信噪比下严格位于支撑集内部。此前该场景下的注水水平仅能通过数值方法获得，缺乏解析闭式解，这与非正方形比例下存在闭式解的情况形成对比，构成了研究缺口。

Method: 采用经典的三角参数化方法对正方形Marčenko-Pastur律进行重构。该方法消除了边缘奇异性，并将原本随信噪比移动的注水截止点转化为单一的角度参数，使得信噪比和容量均成为该角度的显式函数。

Result: 推导出了容量曲线关于角度参数的显式轨迹，无需在每个工作点求解标量约束方程；同时获得了低信噪比下的闭式行为，证明容量在信噪比处的斜率等于极限谱的上边缘。

Conclusion: 该参数化方法填补了正方形MIMO信道容量分析的解析空白，提供了一种通过扫描角度参数而非数值迭代来追踪容量曲线的高效途径，并明确了低信噪比区域的渐近特性。

Abstract: For the canonical independent and identically distributed (IID) single-user multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) channel with full channel state information at the transmitter (CSIT), the asymptotic capacity per receive antenna is a waterfilling integral over the Marčenko-Pastur law, whose shape depends only on the ratio of transmit to receive antennas. Closed forms are known for every ratio other than the square one, and there only above a finite signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). The square ratio behaves differently because the Marčenko-Pastur support reaches the origin. The waterfilling cutoff then stays strictly inside the support at every finite SNR, and the water level has been available only numerically. This letter closes that gap with a classical trigonometric parametrization of the square law, which removes the edge singularity and replaces the moving cutoff by a single angle. Both the SNR and the capacity become explicit functions of that angle, so the capacity curve is traced by sweeping the angle rather than by solving a scalar constraint at each operating point. The same parametrization delivers the low-SNR behavior in closed form, where the slope of capacity in SNR equals the upper edge of the limiting spectrum.

</details>


### [273] [TokenComSR: Task-Sensitivity-Guided Token Communication for Wireless Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03735)
*Ye Wang,Li Qiao,Zhen Gao,Hua Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 针对资源受限无线边缘设备在带宽受限衰落信道下的图像传输问题，提出TokenComSR框架。该框架结合Swin Transformer与超分辨率（SR）模块，通过任务敏感功率分配（TSPA）和信噪比条件令牌细化（TRM）模块，解决了传统分离编码的“悬崖效应”及现有深度联合信源信道编码（JSCC）难以保持块级结构的问题，显著提升了重建保真度和感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分离编码在无线信道中面临“悬崖效应”崩溃问题；现有的基于卷积神经网络的深度联合信源信道编码（JSCC）虽能缓解该问题，但往往无法保持图像的块级（patch-level）结构，导致无法实现自适应的每令牌（per-token）功率分配，并限制了令牌域的超分辨率补偿能力。

Method: 提出TokenComSR框架，核心包含两个模块：1) 任务敏感功率分配（TSPA）模块，将训练阶段的任务敏感度估计蒸馏为推理阶段的令牌功率权重；2) 信噪比（SNR）条件令牌细化（TRM）模块，在解码前估计SNR条件的残差以校正令牌域中的信道失真。该框架将基于Swin Transformer的令牌收发器与接收端SR模块相结合。

Result: 仿真结果证实了TSPA和TRM模块的有效性。与分离编码及现有的JSCC-SR基线方法相比，TokenComSR在图像重建保真度（reconstruction fidelity）和感知质量（perceptual quality）方面均取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: TokenComSR通过引入令牌通信机制与超分辨率技术，有效解决了资源受限无线图像传输中的结构保持与功率分配难题，为带宽受限衰落信道下的高质量图像传输提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: For resource-constrained wireless edge devices over bandwidth-limited fading channels, wireless image transmission using traditional separate coding suffers from the cliff-effect collapse. Prevailing deep joint source-channel coding (JSCC) based on convolutional neural networks can mitigate this issue but usually fail to preserve patch-level structures, thereby preventing adaptive per-token power allocation and limiting token-domain compensation for super-resolution (SR). To address these challenges, we propose a token communication framework with SR (TokenComSR). Specifically, we conceive a task-sensitive power allocation (TSPA) module and a signal-to-noise ratio (SNR)-conditioned token refinement module (TRM). TSPA distills training estimates of task sensitivity into inference token power weights, while TRM estimates an SNR-conditioned residual to correct channel-induced distortion in the token domain before decoding. Building on TSPA and TRM, the proposed TokenComSR pairs a Swin Transformer-based token transceiver with a receiver-side SR module for resource-constrained wireless image transmission. Simulation results confirm the effectiveness of the proposed TSPA and TRM, demonstrating improvements over separate coding and JSCC-SR baselines in both reconstruction fidelity and perceptual quality.

</details>


### [274] [A Quantization Problem Posed by Adaptive Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03745)
*Yuriy A. Reznik*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应码率（ABR）流媒体中的编码阶梯选择问题本质上是标量量化问题，但具有特殊约束：量化单元的重建值固定在左边缘，失真度量是单向质量损失。最优 n 阶梯的质量差距以Θ(1/n) 衰减（而非常规的Θ(1/n²)），并推导出基于带宽密度和质量 - 码率曲线的闭式设计规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 ABR 流媒体中编码阶梯（多码率版本）优化选择的理论问题，现有量化理论无法直接应用，需要建立新的数学模型来指导阶梯设计以最小化质量差距。

Method: 将编码阶梯选择建模为特殊的标量量化问题，分析其独特的约束条件（左边缘重建值、单向质量损失），推导最优阶梯的渐近行为和闭式解，提出基于√(pQ') 密度的阶梯设计法则。

Result: 发现最优 n 阶梯质量差距 G_n* 以Θ(1/n) 衰减；当 n 较大时，n·G_n* 收敛于常数 C_w；推导出量化分位数阶梯放置规则、达到容差ε所需阶梯数 n(ε)≈C_w/ε，以及考虑成本的经济最优阶梯数 n*=√(λC_w/κ)。

Conclusion: 建立了 ABR 编码阶梯优化的新理论框架，提供了闭式设计规则，可推广至不同分辨率、编解码器、感知质量指标及现代 Web 播放器的二维自适应逻辑设计。

Abstract: In adaptive bitrate (ABR) streaming, the delivery technology behind most Internet video, each title is encoded at several bitrates, forming an \emph{encoding ladder} of \emph{renditions}. Each client plays the highest rendition that its network bandwidth can sustain. We show that choosing the ladder is a problem of \emph{scalar quantization} of the bandwidth distribution. However, this quantization problem is of an unusual kind: the client's logic pins each quantization cell's reproduction value to the cell's \emph{left edge}, and the distortion measure is a one-sided quality loss rather than a squared error. The constraint reshapes the classical theory. The \emph{quality gap} of a ladder is the distance between its average delivered quality and the \emph{quality limit} that infinitely many renditions would attain. For optimal $n$-rendition ladders the gap $\mathcal{G}_n^*$ decays as $Θ(1/n)$, not the textbook $Θ(1/n^2)$. Moreover, with large $n$, $n\,\mathcal{G}_n^*\to C_w=\frac{1}{2}(\int\!\sqrt{p\,Q'}\,\,d B)^2$, with the limit attained when the ladder's rates follow the density $\sqrt{p\,Q'}$, which replaces Panter--Dite's $p^{1/3}$; here $p$ is the bandwidth density and $Q$ the quality--rate curve of the content. The law yields closed-form design rules: quantile rung placement, the rendition count $n(\varepsilon)\approx C_w/\varepsilon$ needed to reach tolerance $\varepsilon$, and the economic ladder size $n^*=\sqrt{λC_w/κ}$ when a rendition costs $κ$ to operate and the quality gap is priced at $λ$. The same analysis extends to the design of ladders employing different video resolutions, codecs, perceptual quality metrics, and the two-dimensional adaptation logic of modern web players.

</details>


### [275] [Hierarchical Beam Training and Codebook Design for Movable Antenna-Assisted Near-Field Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03776)
*Meihui Liu,Qian Zhang,Xuejun Cheng,Yuhui Jiao,Yunxiao Li,Ju Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可移动天线（MA）辅助的近场分层波束训练策略，通过设计分层码本，在联合角度 - 距离域形成聚焦波束，显著降低了训练开销并提高了波束增益，性能优于传统固定位置天线（FPA）系统和远场波束训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G 通信系统向更高频段和更大阵列孔径发展，导致近场范围迅速扩大，近场信道估计变得重要且更具挑战性。由于球面波的传播特性，波束训练需要在角度和距离域进行联合搜索，导致训练开销过大。

Method: 基于可移动天线（MA）辅助的近场系统，开发了一种分层波束训练策略，并设计了相应的分层码本。该码本在联合角度 - 距离域形成聚焦波束，最大化目标区域内的波束增益，同时抑制非目标区域的能量泄漏。

Result: 仿真结果证实，该方法相比传统固定位置天线（FPA）系统和远场波束训练方法，具有显著的性能增益。

Conclusion: 利用可移动天线技术的空间变化特性，通过分层波束训练策略和分层码本设计，有效解决了近场波束训练开销大的问题，实现了低开销与高波束增益的平衡。

Abstract: As sixth-generation (6G) communication systems evolve toward higher frequency bands and larger array apertures, the near-field range expands rapidly, making near-field channel estimation increasingly important and challenging. Beam training has been recognized as an effective approach for channel state information (CSI) acquisition. However, because of the propagation characteristics of spherical waves, beam training needs to perform a joint search in the angle and distance domains, which results in unaffordable beam training overhead. By flexibly reconfiguring antenna positions, movable antenna (MA) technology can fully exploit the spatial variations of wireless channels and achieve more accurate beam focusing, thereby providing additional flexibility for efficient beam training design. Therefore, based on MA-assisted near-field systems, we develop a hierarchical beam training strategy that combines reduced training overhead with high beam gain and design a corresponding hierarchical codebook. This codebook forms focused beams over the joint angle-distance domain, maximizing beam gain within the target region while suppressing energy leakage into non-target regions. Simulation results confirm substantial performance gains of the method over the conventional fixed-position antenna (FPA) system and the far-field beam training method.

</details>


### [276] [On the Impact of Site-Specific Training for a Real-World 5G NR System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04004)
*Reinhard Wiesmayr,Nuri Berke Baytekin,Chris Dick,Christoph Studer*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了在5G NR测试床上对三种接收器架构（全训练神经网络、模型驱动神经网络和基于模型）进行站点特定微调的效果，发现微调能显著提升前两种接收器性能，且在不同时间跨度的测量中保持有效，同时站点特定信道估计结合迭代检测解码可实现最低误码率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 站点特定训练可提升无线接收器性能且不增加计算复杂度，但现有研究多集中于全训练神经接收器和单层传输，缺乏对多种接收器架构及实际多层传输场景的验证。

Method: 在ETH Zurich的标准合规5G NR测试床上进行双上行链路传输测量，对三种接收器架构进行站点特定微调，并评估其在不同传输配置和时间跨度下的性能；同时研究基于合成信道或站点特定测量估计协方差矩阵的站点特定线性最小均方误差信道估计方法。

Result: 站点特定微调显著提升全训练和模型驱动神经接收器性能，对基于模型接收器提升有限；单一神经接收器经联合微调后可匹配分别微调的性能；微调效果在相隔六个月以上的测量中仍有效；站点特定信道估计结合迭代检测解码实现数据集中最低误码率。

Conclusion: 站点特定微调是一种有效且持久的方法，可显著提升神经接收器在真实5G场景中的性能，尤其适用于多层传输配置，且站点特定信道估计能进一步优化系统误码率表现。

Abstract: Site-specific training can improve wireless receiver performance without increasing computational complexity. However, real-world results have so far focused on fully trainable neural receivers and single-layer transmissions. We study site-specific finetuning of three receiver architectures: fully trainable neural, model-driven neural, and model-based. We train and evaluate these receivers using new measurements from a standard-compliant 5G NR testbed at ETH Zurich with dual-layer uplink transmission, including measurement campaigns conducted more than six months apart. Our results show that site-specific finetuning (i) substantially improves fully trainable and model-driven neural receivers, while resulting in only marginal gains for the less tunable model-based receiver; (ii) enables a single neural receiver jointly finetuned for single- and dual-layer transmission to closely match receivers finetuned separately for each configuration; and (iii) remains effective across measurement campaigns separated by more than six months. We also investigate site-specific linear minimum mean-square error channel estimation using covariance matrices estimated from either synthetic channels or site-specific measurements. When combined with iterative detection and decoding, site-specific channel estimation achieves the lowest error rate observed in our datasets. Our finetuning code and measurement datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/IIP-Group/site_specific_training

</details>


### [277] [Synchronization Strings over the Optimal Alphabet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04122)
*Huibo Xu,Shi Fu,Youming Qiao,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文解决了同步字符串的三元组情况，证明了三个符号是精确的最小常数字母表大小，并提出了局部熵转移定理和计算机辅助优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决同步字符串字母表大小的开放问题，特别是确定三元组情况下是否存在任意长度的同步字符串，以及找到最小常数字母表大小。

Method: 提出局部熵转移定理，使用Brinkhuis家族中的发生分支实例化该定理，结合删除球估计和非对称Lovasz局部引理来同时满足所有区间约束。

Result: 证明了每个长度都存在三元2001/2002-同步字符串，通过计算机辅助优化得到每个ε>226/227的三元ε-同步字符串，并产生了指数级有效词、拉斯维加斯多项式时间构造等结果。

Conclusion: 三个符号是同步字符串的精确最小常数字母表大小，所提出的框架不仅解决了三元组情况，还提供了多种构造方法和应用。

Abstract: Synchronization strings attach deterministic position labels to a stream that survive insertions and deletions. Haeupler and Shahrasbi introduced these objects, and subsequent work proved that four symbols suffice for some fixed parameter epsilon < 1, whereas two symbols cannot support arbitrarily long synchronization strings. We resolve the remaining ternary case: every length admits a ternary 2001/2002-synchronization string. Thus three is the exact minimum constant alphabet size. A computer-assisted refinement based on a larger 54-uniform family yields ternary epsilon-synchronization strings for every epsilon > 226/227.
  The previous four-symbol construction uses a ternary square-free backbone to exclude short repetitions and a fourth symbol to carry long-range synchronization marks. Our main technical contribution is a local-entropy transfer theorem: every square-free block-local source with positive conditional min-entropy supports synchronization strings with a fixed gap. We instantiate this theorem using occurrence-wise branching in a Brinkhuis family. Every outcome remains ternary and square-free, while every long interval retains linear conditional min-entropy after all choices outside it are exposed. A deletion-ball estimate converts this entropy into anti-concentration for high longest common subsequences, and an asymmetric Lovasz Local Lemma enforces all interval constraints simultaneously. The same framework also yields exponentially many valid words, a Las Vegas polynomial-time construction, synchronization circles, and synchronization within a class of extremal square-free words.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [278] [Learning from Scarce Labels: Multi-View Echocardiography for Ejection Fraction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02969)
*Zhiyuan Gao,Dominic Yurk,Yaser S. Abu-Mostafa*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文首次公开了基于胸骨旁长轴（PLAX）超声心动图预测左室射血分数（EF）的资源，通过创新的数据生成策略构建了包含超过 25,000 个 PLAX 视频的数据集，训练出的模型平均绝对误差（MAE）为 6.86%，结合心尖四腔（A4C）视图的多视图融合进一步将 MAE 降至 6.37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏基于 PLAX 视图的 EF 预测数据集，且在某些临床情况下无法获取标准的心尖四腔（A4C）视图，因此需要开发一种基于 PLAX 视图的 EF 预测方法以补充现有临床标准。

Method: 利用临床笔记与超声心动图视频之间的时间相关性，结合视图分类器的微调和代理标签（proxy labeling）策略，生成了超过 25,000 个标记的 PLAX 视频数据集，并训练了可复现的 PLAX EF 预测模型，最后通过简单的无权重后期融合（late fusion）结合 PLAX 和 A4C 预测结果。

Result: PLAX EF 模型的平均绝对误差（MAE）为 6.86%，与临床标准的 A4C 方法（MAE 6%-7%）相当；PLAX 与 A4C 多视图融合后 MAE 降至 6.37%，优于单一视图基线。

Conclusion: 基于 PLAX 视图的 EF 估计在技术上可行且具有临床意义，尤其在无法获取 A4C 视图的情况下提供了有效的替代方案；多视图整合能进一步提升预测性能，相关数据集、模型和演示已开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: We present, to the best of our knowledge, the first publicly available resource for predicting left ventricular ejection fraction (EF) from parasternal long-axis (PLAX) echocardiography. Because no PLAX-EF datasets previously existed, our work focuses on an innovative data generation strategy to overcome this scarcity. By leveraging a time-based correlation between clinical notes and echocardiographic videos, combined with fine-tuning view classifiers and proxy labeling, we created a labeled dataset of over 25,000 PLAX videos. This enables us to train the first reproducible PLAX EF model, achieving a mean absolute error (MAE) of 6.86%. Given that apical four-chamber (A4C) methods, the clinical standard, report MAE values of 6%-7%, our results demonstrate that EF estimation from PLAX views is both feasible and clinically relevant. This surpasses the performance of existing methods and provides a clinically relevant solution for situations where apical views may not be feasible. Going further, we demonstrate that combining PLAX and A4C predictions via simple unweighted late fusion improves both single-view baselines to a 6.37% MAE, underscoring the value of multi-view integration. To promote continued research, we release the dataset labels, trained models, and runnable demos on GitHub, Hugging Face, and Google Colab: https://github.com/Jeffrey4899/PLAX_EF_Labels_202509

</details>


### [279] [Beyond Blur: A Semantic Tri-view Pipeline for Teledermatology Gradability via Skin Micro-relief](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03095)
*Robert Engel*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“语义三视图管道”的可解释架构，通过将表皮微浮雕作为图像质量的可计算生物标志物，利用多视角冗余自动筛选远程皮肤病学的照片分级能力，在明确案例上实现了0.96的AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机皮肤照片对远程皮肤病学至关重要，但评估提交病例的诊断适用性（分级能力）仍是移动护理工作流程中的关键瓶颈。皮肤科医生通常需审查多个摄影视角以识别一致的纹理细节，而现有方法难以自动化这一过程。

Method: 提出语义三视图管道（Semantic Tri-view Pipeline），利用专家标注的SCIN数据集子集训练轻量级DeepLabV3+模型以分割微浮雕保真度，随后通过逻辑回归分类器聚合多达三个病例视图的空间掩膜，利用视角冗余增强鲁棒性，并学习如“惩罚区域距离视图中的高保真纹理”等临床可理解的启发式规则。

Result: 在预设90%灵敏度操作点下，系统在SCIN数据集上的表现从方差较大的多数共识案例的0.81 AUC（80.6% PPV）提升至光学明确的一致案例的0.96 AUC（97.7% PPV）；系统误差主要反映边缘案例中临床医生的主观差异及对非视觉元数据的依赖。

Conclusion: 该研究交付了一种可解释、隐私设计内建且适用于边缘计算的实时反馈系统，能在病例提交阶段过滤不可分级的照片集，从而优化远程皮肤病学的工作流程。

Abstract: Smartphone skin photographs are indispensable to teledermatology, yet assessing the diagnostic suitability of submitted cases (gradability) remains a critical bottleneck in mobile care workflows. Dermatologists routinely review multiple photographic views (regional, angled, and close-up) to identify consistent textural detail rather than relying on a single image. We present the Semantic Tri-view Pipeline, an interpretable architecture for automated teledermatology gradability screening that formalizes epidermal micro-relief as a computable biomarker of image quality. Using an expert-annotated subset of the public SCIN dataset, we train a lightweight DeepLabV3+ model to segment micro-relief fidelity. These spatial masks are then aggregated across up to three case views with a logistic regression classifier, leveraging viewpoint redundancy to support robustness under uncontrolled smartphone acquisition. This approach learns context-aware, clinically intelligible heuristics, such as penalizing high-fidelity texture in regional distance views. Evaluated at a predefined 90% sensitivity operating point, the system's apparent errors largely reflect subjective clinical variance on borderline cases where clinicians rely on non-visual metadata. On SCIN, performance improves from an AUC of 0.81 (80.6% PPV) on variance-heavy majority-consensus cases to 0.96 (97.7% PPV) on optically unambiguous unanimous cases. Overall, this work delivers an interpretable, privacy-by-design, edge-ready system that can provide real-time feedback during case submission to filter ungradable photo sets before review.

</details>


### [280] [Plant Growth Estimation with a Camera-Based Vegetation Index Mapping System for Agricultural Ground Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03872)
*Lukas Pindl,Michael Maier,Timo Oksanen*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于摄像头的传感器系统，利用RTK-GNSS和透视投影技术，在低端硬件上实现农田植物生长的实时多光谱地图构建，其NDVI趋势与商用传感器一致，且具备过滤非地面区域的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在开发一种低成本、可实时处理的农业监测方案，以解决现有商用设备成本高或计算复杂度大，难以在低端硬件上实时生成大范围植物生长地图的问题。

Method: 采用搭载于地面车辆（如拖拉机）的摄像头，结合RTK-GNSS定位技术将多张多光谱图像融合；利用透视投影将图像映射到估计的地面平面，计算NDVI指数，并实施地面与非地面区域的过滤算法。

Result: 该系统可在低端硬件上实现实时图像处理；与商用车辆挂载传感器相比，虽然绝对数值存在差异，但两者显示的生长趋势高度一致；该方法能有效过滤非地面区域，降低作物密度对平均NDVI测量的干扰。

Conclusion: 基于摄像头的传感器方案是一种计算简单、成本效益高且有效的农业监测工具，能够实时生成准确的植物生长趋势图，并通过区域过滤优化测量精度，具有替代或补充现有商用传感器的潜力。

Abstract: This work presents a camera-based sensor for mapping plant growth over an agricultural field. The sensor can be used on ground-based vehicles like a tractor and relies on RTK-GNSS to correctly merge many multispectral images onto one map. NDVI is used as an index to estimate plant growth, but the approach can be used with other indices as well depending on the camera. The images from the camera are projected onto the estimated ground plane using perspective projection. This computationally simple approach allows for real-time processing of all images on the field even with low-end hardware. The results are compared to a commercially established vehicle-mounted sensor. Absolute values are hard to compare, but both sensors show similar trends. The camera-based approach also allows for filtering of ground and non-ground areas, potentially reducing the impact of crop density on the average measured NDVI.

</details>


### [281] [Computationally Efficient Pathology Segmentation using Knowledge Distillation from Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03947)
*Jiaqi Lv,Yijie Zhu,Saki Okada,Maxwell Stanley Renna,Robert Goldin,Stefan Antonowicz,Shan E Ahmed Raza*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于基础模型知识蒸馏的框架，旨在解决组织病理学全切片图像（WSI）中像素级分割标注成本高、自然图像预训练模型迁移效果差以及病理基础模型部署计算开销大的问题。该方法通过将Virchow2教师模型的知识迁移至紧凑的学生网络，实现了在大幅减少参数量和提升推理速度的同时，保持甚至超越最先进（SOTA）的分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动组织分割对于大规模病理分析至关重要，但面临三大挑战：1) 像素级标注获取成本高昂；2) 基于自然图像预训练的模型在病理领域迁移效果不佳；3) 现有的病理基础模型虽然表征能力强，但计算成本高，难以大规模部署。

Method: 提出了一种基础模型知识蒸馏框架。首先，基于Virchow2训练分割教师模型（包含LoRA适配变体），在PUMA、IGNITE、BEETLE及私有血管分割数据集上达到SOTA或极具竞争力的性能。随后，将这些教师模型的响应级（response-level）和特征级（feature-level）知识蒸馏到紧凑的学生网络中。

Result: 在四个数据集上的实验表明，蒸馏方法显著提升了学生网络的性能，优于仅使用监督训练的效果。生成的学生模型实现了SOTA或接近SOTA的分割精度，同时参数量大幅减少，推理吞吐量相比基于基础模型的分割网络提高了高达10倍。

Conclusion: 基础模型的知识可以有效迁移至高效的分割模型中，从而在不牺牲性能的前提下实现可扩展的大规模部署。基于公开数据集训练的模型将通过TIAToolbox发布。

Abstract: Automatic tissue segmentation is essential for large-scale analysis of histopathology whole-slide images (WSIs), but accurate pixel-level segmentation remains challenging. Pixel-level annotations are expensive to obtain, models pre-trained on natural images may transfer poorly to histopathology, and pathology foundation models, despite their strong representations, are computationally expensive to deploy at scale. We address these challenges with a foundation-model knowledge distillation framework for efficient tissue segmentation. We first train Virchow2-based segmentation teachers, including a LoRA-adapted variant, achieving state-of-the-art or highly competitive performance across four datasets: PUMA, IGNITE, BEETLE, and a private blood vessel segmentation dataset. We then transfer response-level and feature-level knowledge from these teachers into compact student networks. Distillation consistently improves student performance over supervised training alone, producing state-of-the-art or near state-of-the-art results with substantially fewer parameters and up to ten-fold higher inference throughput than foundation-model-based segmentation networks. These results show that foundation-model knowledge can be effectively transferred to efficient segmentation models for scalable deployment without compromising performance. Models trained on the public datasets will be released through TIAToolbox.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

### [282] [Learning Multiband Signals and Fourier-sparse Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02977)
*Dongrun Cai,Xue Chen,Xiaowei Shao*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种高效算法：一是能在未知频段位置的情况下，以近最优的样本和时间复杂度恢复多带信号的频段位置；二是通过将$k$-傅里叶稀疏信号近似为多带信号，实现了样本复杂度为$\tilde{O}(k^2)$（达到统计上界）的插值算法，尽管时间复杂度较高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多带信号重建算法通常假设频段位置已知，缺乏在未知位置下高效恢复频段的算法；同时，尽管傅里叶稀疏信号学习历史悠久，但设计兼具高效性与鲁棒性的算法仍是开放性问题，且现有最优算法的样本复杂度$\tilde{O}(k^3)$尚未达到统计上界$\tilde{O}(k^2)$。

Method: 1. 提出一种新算法，直接在$\ell_2$有界噪声环境下，以$\tilde{O}(n+\sum_i |I_i|)$的样本和时间复杂度恢复多带信号的频段位置，并结合Avron等人的算法实现完整插值。
2. 证明任意$k$-傅里叶稀疏信号均可被支持集大小为$\tilde{O}(k^2)$的多带信号近似，并基于此近似构建插值算法。

Result: 1. 实现了多带信号频段位置的高效恢复，总样本和时间复杂度均为$\tilde{O}(n+\sum_i |I_i|)$。
2. 构建了$k$-傅里叶稀疏信号的插值算法，将样本复杂度降低至$\tilde{O}(k^2)$（匹配统计上界），时间复杂度为$\tilde{O}(k^5)$。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了多带信号在未知频段位置下的学习空白，并通过多带近似策略显著降低了傅里叶稀疏信号学习的样本复杂度，使其达到理论最优界限，推动了信号处理领域高效算法的发展。

Abstract: We consider efficient algorithms to learn multiband signals and Fourier-sparse signals. A mutliband signal has a Fourier transform supported by a bounded number of intervals, say $I_1 \cup I_2 \cdots \cup I_n$. There is a long line of research on multiband signals. In particular, Avron et al. showed an efficient reconstructing algorithm whose sample complexity is almost optimal. However, all previous algorithms for multiband signals consider the reconstructing problem in which the locations of $I_1,\ldots,I_n$ are given as a priori knowledge. On the other hand, although the problem of learning Fourier-sparse signals with $k$ arbitrary frequencies dates at least to Prony in 1795, designing efficient and robust learning algorithms is still an open problem. The state-of-the-art is an efficient algorithm of $\tilde{O}(k^3)$ samples and $\tilde{O}(k^{3 ω})$ time from the very recent work by Cai et al., while the statistical upper bound is $\tilde{O}(k^2)$ samples.
  Let $[-1,1]$ be the time window in which the noise is $\ell_2$ bounded.
  1. We show an efficient algorithm to recover the locations of the bands $I_1,\ldots,I_n$ in $\hat{x}$ within $\tilde{O}(n+\sum_i |I_i|)$ samples and $\tilde{O}(n+\sum_i |I_i|)$ time. Furthermore, combining this with the reconstructing algorithm by Avron et al. provides an efficient interpolation algorithm within $\tilde{O}(n+\sum_i |I_i|)$ samples.
  2. We show that every $k$-Fourier-sparse signal $x$ admits a multiband approximation $z$ whose Fourier transform is of support size $|\mathrm{supp}(\hat{z})|=\tilde{O}(k^2)$. Furthermore, we show an interpolation algorithm for $k$-Fourier-sparse signals with $\tilde{O}(k^2)$ samples and $\tilde{O}(k^5)$ time.

</details>


### [283] [SparseStack Is an Optimal Oblivious Subspace Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02978)
*Diar Heidary*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了完全独立的 SparseStack 草图在特定参数下是 oblivious subspace embedding，其行数和稀疏度达到了 Nelson 和 Nguyen (2013)  conjectured 的最优界，证明过程通过矩界分析和算子理论完成，并已在 Lean 4 中形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 Nelson 和 Nguyen (2013) 提出的关于 SparseStack 草图构造参数的猜想，即确定其作为 oblivious subspace embedding 所需的最优行数 $m$ 和稀疏度 $s$，以填补理论空白并提供显式常数。

Method: 通过界定 Gram 误差的偶数矩进行证明。采用条件期望耦合技术，将符号 one-hot 列选择器替换为具有独立三点分布的向量。利用三点分布的三维 $L_2$ 空间性质，将矩计算转化为有限张量积上确定性算子的真空矩阵元，并通过分级算子带分析和共享因子不等式（shared-factor inequality）进行界限估计，最后结合 $2q$ 步展开和马尔可夫不等式完成论证。

Result: 证明了当 $m = O((d+\log(1/δ))/ε^2)$ 且 $s = O(\log(d/δ)/ε)$ 时，SparseStack 草图能以失真 $ε$ 和失败概率 $δ$ 嵌入 $d$ 维子空间。该结果的行数达到了理论下界的最优性，且证明了显式常数。该定理已在 Lean 4 中得到形式化验证。

Conclusion: 该研究确认了 SparseStack 构造的最优参数猜想，提供了一种不依赖高斯比较的有限维证明方法，并展示了在 AI 辅助下开发并经形式化验证的数学证明的可行性。
