In recent incidents, agent swarms coordinated covertly through improvised side-channels (Dalton and Wallace, 2026; Greenblatt et al., 2026). Our setting differs: the same transparent channels that carried the exploit also gave non-cheating agents the visibility they needed to detect fraud, organize resistance, and enforce norms. We cast the problem of managing the agents' shared infrastructure as the knowledge commons governance problem (Ostrom, 1990). To protect the commons from exploits, we propose to adopt institutional mechanisms, such as graduated sanctioning and collective-choice rules, to support decentralized self-governance in autonomous swarms.

</details>


### [356] [Rethinking On-Policy Distillation of Large Language Models II: One Training Example](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04172)
*Zixuan Fu,Bingxiang He,Yuxin Zuo,Haohuan Huang,Jinqian Zhang,Ruhang Xiao,Cheng Qian,Qinyu Luo,Huan-ang Gao,Yudong Wang,Zhiyuan Liu,Ning Ding,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: On-policy蒸馏（OPD）在数据极小化极限下（单查询训练）仍能恢复大部分全数据OPD的性能增益，表明OPD是数据过载但算法饥饿的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPD研究主要关注算法行为，但训练数据的作用尚不明确，需要探究数据在OPD中的实际影响。

Method: 通过在单查询上训练OPD，测量状态覆盖率（state coverage）和学生与教师的对齐速率，分析不同查询数量对覆盖率和验证准确率的影响。

Result: 单查询可达71.5%状态覆盖率，16个语义不同查询可达98.9%并匹配全数据训练；对齐速度在单查询和全数据集上相似，学生吸收监督的速度逐渐变慢。

Conclusion: OPD的rollouts能快速暴露广泛监督，但学生吸收监督的速度越来越慢，未来工作应关注OPD的步骤效率，重新审视数据和其成功背后的机制。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) combines student-generated rollouts with dense token-level supervision from a teacher. Existing work has mainly studied its algorithmic behavior, leaving the role of training data unclear. We examine this role at the data-minimal limit by training on a single query. One-shot OPD keeps improving for hundreds of steps and recovers most of full-data OPD's gain across task domains and model families. We explain this result through the states visited during training and the rate at which the student aligns with the teacher. We measure \emph{state coverage}, the fraction of the states full-data OPD visits that a query set's rollouts reach. A single query already reaches \(71.5\%\), most of it within the first 100 steps. Adding semantically distinct queries raises coverage and validation accuracy together, until 16 queries reach \(98.9\%\) and match full-data training. Yet alignment slows at a similar pace whether OPD trains on one query or the whole dataset, and even a fixed set of states takes hundreds of steps to absorb. OPD is therefore data-overfed but algorithm-starved. Its rollouts quickly expose broad supervision, while the student absorbs that supervision increasingly slowly. The state-coverage result extends to multi-teacher OPD, where 16 semantically diverse queries per domain match full-data MOPD. As a further stress test, content-light templates and off-domain WildChat queries also approach the real-query baseline. Task content and induced state coverage can therefore come apart. We hope these findings direct future work toward the step efficiency of OPD, and prompt a re-examination of the data and the mechanisms behind its recent successes in frontier post-training.

</details>


### [357] [A Computationally Feasible Framework for Causal Probabilistic Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04177)
*Rafal Urbaniak,Sam Witty,Daniel Waxman,Andy Zane,Poorva Garg,Emily Bunnapradist,Sankaran Vaidyanathan,Jack Feser,Drew Lehe,Eli Bingham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了概率因果影响（PCI）方法，旨在解决现有实际因果性（AC）理论难以扩展至大型模型，而SHAP等可扩展归因方法又忽略因果结构的问题。PCI通过将可解释性问题重构为概率因果模型上的估计问题，利用蒙特卡洛近似，实现了既具备因果理论基础又具有计算可扩展性的分级解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的归因工具存在两极分化：实际因果性（AC）理论虽然能提供原则性的判断，但因需枚举反事实场景而仅适用于小型模型；可扩展的归因方法（如SHAP）则部分忽略了生成数据的因果结构，可能导致与严谨因果分析相冲突的结果。研究旨在填补这一空白，提供一种既能处理复杂模型又能保持因果一致性的方法。

Method: 提出概率因果影响（PCI）框架。该方法基于实际因果性和Pearl的必要性及充分性概率概念，将可解释性重构为概率因果模型上的估计问题。通过定义“候选解释”的分布、反事实值的分布以及评分函数，利用蒙特卡洛方法进行近似计算，从而获得可处理的、基于因果的分级解释。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界案例中进行了评估，包括与实际因果性（AC）的一致性检查、扩展性实验、复杂连续值动态系统分析，以及在基于数百万数据点训练的真实部署因果机器学习模型上的应用，验证了该方法的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: PCI成功弥合了理论因果性与可扩展归因方法之间的差距，作为实际因果性和Pearl因果概率的广义形式，它能够提供适用于大规模复杂系统的、具有因果依据的量化解释。

Abstract: Explaining why a specific outcome occurred, and which inputs deserve the blame or credit, is central to philosophical, scientific, and policy analysis. Existing tools split into two camps. The theory of actual causality (AC) gives principled verdicts, but only for toy-sized models, because computing them requires enumerating counterfactual scenarios. Scalable attribution methods like SHAP (or even causal SHAP) at least partially ignore the causal structure that generated the data, and can give answers that conflict with a careful causal analysis. We close this gap with Probabilistic Causal Impact (PCI).
  PCI builds on actual causality and on Pearl's notions of probability of necessity and sufficiency, but recasts the question of explainability as an estimation problem on a probabilistic causal model that is easily approximated via Monte Carlo. By specifying a distribution over "candidate explanations," a distribution over counterfactual values, and a scoring function, PCI provides tractable, causally grounded, graded explanations, generalizing AC and Pearl's probability of causation as degenerate cases.
  We evaluate PCI in synthetic and real-world examples, spanning consistency checks with AC, scaling experiments, complex continuous-valued dynamical systems, and a real-world deployed causal machine learning model trained on millions of datapoints.

</details>


### [358] [Clean Engineering, Unstable Measurement: A Preregistered Reliability Failure of Black-Box LLM Observers on Shared Endpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04198)
*Haoyaun Zhu,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过两次预注册审计发现，作为评估工具的语言模型裁判存在严重的不稳定性（同一请求在不同时间或重复发送时结果不一致），其一致性远低于测量所需的阈值，导致其无法作为可靠的冻结评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型裁判目前被广泛用于筛选训练数据、评分生成内容及驱动排行榜，但其有效性基于一个鲜被陈述的假设：即同一请求在相同模型名下，其评估结果在不同时间是恒定不变的。本研究旨在审计这一关键假设的有效性。

Method: 进行了两次预注册审计活动，预先固定所有阈值。在共享服务基础设施上，对52,988次请求尝试进行了外部行为测量，包括同一窗口内的重复排名测试和次日字节级完全相同的重放测试，并分析了标签映射偏差、候选差距及输入噪声等机制。

Result: 审计结果显示，同一窗口内的重复排名Spearman相关系数仅为0.400（要求0.90），次日字节级重放的一致性为0.78（要求0.99）。主要问题源于标签到意义的映射偏差、远低于仪器噪声底限的候选差距，以及字节级相同输入返回不同排名的噪声。等待时间、切换提供商或自托管均未能有效解决该问题。

Conclusion: 在共享端点上，模型名称并非冻结的测量仪器。预注册评估必须在冻结任何网关标准之前，先对其测量仪器本身进行验证。研究提出了三级快照身份阶梯、八条设计规则及报告清单，以解决此类不可靠的评估问题。

Abstract: Language-model judges now gate training data, score generations, and drive leaderboards. The judge is then a measurement instrument, resting on one rarely stated assumption: the same request, sent to the same model name, reads the same tomorrow. We audited that assumption in two preregistered campaigns with every threshold fixed in advance; neither got past validating its instrument. Across 52,988 audited request attempts, same-window repeat rankings agreed at Spearman 0.400 against a required 0.90, and byte-identical next-day replays agreed at 0.78 against a required 0.99, each time with the execution record at ceiling. Three mechanisms explain the gap: a label-to-meaning mapping that biased readouts as strongly as the signal; candidate gaps seven orders of magnitude below the instrument's own noise floor; and byte-identical inputs returning different rankings, a noise that exact-permutation readouts compound. Neither metric substitution nor sampling repaired it on the tested grid. Preregistered follow-ups bound the problem: waiting did not help on the days sampled (0.805 versus 0.800, replicated over five further days); switching providers did not help (four providers share the floor, medians 0.74 to 0.88, predicted by none of the metadata fields they expose); self-hosting on batch-invariant kernels helped only while the server was quiet; and on constructed errors with known gaps, the readout's separation tracks error type, not size. We distill the evidence into a three-level snapshot-identity ladder, eight design rules, and a reporting checklist; a pilot at roughly 2% of the study's call volume would have exposed both unreachable gates in advance. All results concern externally measured behaviour on shared serving infrastructure. On a shared endpoint, a model name is not a frozen instrument; a preregistered evaluation must measure its instrument before freezing any gate on it.

</details>


<div id='cs.CC'></div>

# cs.CC [[Back]](#toc)

### [359] [Promise Systems of Equations over Magmas with Identity and over Algebras in Congruence Modular Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03469)
*Nick Jamesson*

Main category: cs.CC

TL;DR: 本文研究有限代数上承诺方程系统的计算复杂性，推广了前人结果，获得了包含所有幺半群的代数类上的P-NP难二分性结果，以及同余模簇代数上的二分性结果，并给出了同余模簇代数上承诺方程系统元问题的准多项式时间算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究承诺方程系统问题的计算复杂性分类，特别是推广已有结果到更广泛的代数类，并解决元问题的算法复杂性。

Method: 推广Larrauri等人的结果，利用代数结构理论分析承诺方程系统在同余模簇和包含幺半群的代数类上的复杂性，设计准多项式时间算法解决元问题。

Result: 获得了包含所有幺半群的代数类上的P-NP难二分性结果，同余模簇代数上的二分性结果，以及同余模簇代数上承诺方程系统元问题的准多项式时间算法。

Conclusion: 承诺方程系统问题在特定代数类上存在明确的复杂性二分性，且元问题可在准多项式时间内判定是否属于P类。

Abstract: We study the computational complexity of solving promise systems of equations over finite algebras. Given two algebras $\mathbf{A}$ and $\mathbf{B}$ with a homomorphism from $\mathbf{A}$ to $\mathbf{B}$, the promise system of equations problem is to determine if an input system of equations has a solution in $\mathbf{A}$ or not even in $\mathbf{B}$. We generalize the results of Larrauri, Mottet, and Živný [ACM ToCL'26] to obtain a $\mathbf{P}-\mathbf{NP}$-hard dichotomy result for promise systems of equations over a class of algebras which contains all monoids, and a dichotomy result for promise systems of equations over algebras in a congruence modular variety. We then consider the metaproblem for promise systems of equations over algebras in a congruence modular variety: given finite algebras $\mathbf{A}$ and $\mathbf{B}$ such that $\mathbf{A}$ is in a congruence modular variety, we show there is a quasi-polynomial time algorithm for determining whether or not the associated promise system of equations problem is in $\mathbf{P}$.

</details>


### [360] [Random Garbage Separates XOR from Forward-Only Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03628)
*Khaled Elbassioni,Rishikesh Gajjala,Saurabh Ray*

Main category: cs.CC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在标准XOR接口与无前向逆/伴随接口之间存在指数级的量子查询分离，解决了Scott Aaronson提出的开放性问题11，并构建了相应的置换问题实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在回答Scott Aaronson在2021年提出的关于量子查询复杂度的开放问题11，即探究在缺乏逆 oracle 或伴随 oracle 的前向接口（forward-only interfaces）下，量子算法的查询复杂度是否会显著高于标准XOR接口。

Method: 构造了一个基于函数 $f_{h,r}(x)=(h(x),x,r_x)$ 的问题实例，其中 $h$ 承诺为置换或Simon二对一函数，$r$ 为固定随机标签表。通过分析仅记录替换（analysis-only recording replacement）技术，追踪固定随机标签表在 $T$ 次调用后的算子贡献，利用随机置换与随机Simon函数在包含隐藏Simon对时的概率差异 $O(T^2/N)$ 来推导下界。此外，还将该实例嵌入到大小为 $L=4N^2$ 的置换域中。

Result: 1. 该问题在标准XOR查询下仅需 $n+2$ 次查询即可解决，但在前向擦除（forward-erasing）查询下的复杂度为 $Θ(\sqrt N)$，实现了指数级分离。
2. 嵌入置换后，标准查询上界保持不变，而前向原位（in-place）查询复杂度为 $Θ(\sqrt N)=Θ(L^{1/4})$。
3. 即使存在干净的相干旁路（clean coherent bypass），上述下界依然成立。

Conclusion: 研究证实了在没有逆或伴随 oracle 的前向接口模型中，量子查询复杂度确实存在指数级的劣势，从而肯定了开放问题11的猜想，并通过严谨的算子分析确立了该分离的下界有效性。

Abstract: We give exponential quantum query separations between the standard XOR interface and two forward-only interfaces that supply neither an adjoint nor an inverse oracle. Let $X=\F_2^n$, $N=|X|$, and $f_{h,r}(x)=(h(x),x,r_x)$, where $h:X\to X$ is promised to be either a permutation or a Simon two-to-one function, and $r$ is a fixed table of $n$-bit tags, unrestricted by the promise and reused on every query. The resulting problem is solvable with at most $n+2$ standard XOR queries, but has forward-erasing query complexity $Θ(\sqrt N)$. This answers affirmatively open question 11 in [Scott Aaronson. Open problems related to quantum query complexity. ACM Transactions on Quantum Computing, 2(4):14:1-14:9, 2021] . We also embed these instances into permutations. The detailed construction retains the copy of $x$ in each prescribed output, but for these promises that copy can be replaced by one bit that distinguishes the two inputs in every Simon pair. This gives a permutation domain of size $L=4N^2$ and a permutation problem with the same standard-query upper bound and forward-only in-place query complexity $Θ(\sqrt N)=Θ(L^{1/4})$. Both lower bounds remain valid with a clean coherent bypass. The common lower bound uses an analysis-only recording replacement. In the replacement computation, tracing out the fixed random tag table after $T$ calls gives a sum of positive-semidefinite operator contributions, each depending on $h$ at no more than $T$ addresses. On such a set, the restrictions induced by random permutations and random Simon functions differ only if the set contains a hidden Simon pair, an event of probability $O(T^2/N)$.

</details>


### [361] [The Head Complexity of Boolean Functions in Single-Layer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04046)
*Rajmohan Rajaraman,Ravi Sundaram,Amanuel Tesfaye*

Main category: cs.CC

TL;DR: 本文研究了单层纯注意力模型中计算函数所需的最少注意力头数量（头复杂度），建立了精确的层级关系：$k$个头可计算$k$位奇偶校验但无法计算$(k+1)$位；证明了无界嵌入维度和数值精度无法替代注意力头的作用，并给出了通用布尔函数的近乎匹配的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究单层自注意力机制的计算能力边界，特别是明确“注意力头数量”这一核心资源在计算布尔函数时的具体作用，厘清其与嵌入维度、数值精度等其他资源之间的替代关系。

Method: 通过构造“交替和阻碍”（alternating-sum obstruction）证明下界：在消除softmax分母后，生成的决策多项式中的每一项都至少缺失一个输入位，导致其与奇偶校验函数的相关性为零。同时利用紧致性定理（compactness theorem）和计数论证（counting argument）分别确立资源边界和通用函数的复杂度界限。

Result: 1. 确立了头复杂度的精确层级：$k$个头能计算$k$位奇偶校验，但不能计算$(k+1)$位，且该下界在无界嵌入维度和精度下依然成立。2. 证明了紧致性：任何可计算函数均可由受限于任务离散数据（头数、字母表大小、长度）的维度和精度实现，表明无界资源无法替代头。3. 得出通用二元函数的界限：$2^n$个头足以计算所有$n$位函数，而几乎所有函数需要$\Omega(2^n/n^2)$个头，上下界仅在多项式因子内存在差距。

Conclusion: 研究结果完整刻画了单层注意力模型中布尔计算对注意力头数量的需求，证明了注意力头是独立且不可替代的计算资源，无限制的嵌入维度或数值精度无法弥补头数量的不足。

Abstract: What can a single layer of self-attention compute? We study head complexity: the minimum number of attention heads required to compute a function in a one-layer attention-only model. We establish an exact hierarchy under this measure: $k$ heads compute $k$-bit parity but cannot compute $(k+1)$-bit parity.
  The lower bound is unconditional in the two resources a transformer might otherwise exploit; it holds at unbounded embedding dimension and unbounded numerical precision. The proof rests on an alternating-sum obstruction: after clearing the softmax denominators, every monomial in the resulting decision polynomial omits at least one of the $k+1$ input bits, forcing its correlation with parity to vanish. The same obstruction yields lower bounds for related tasks, including the well-studied multi-hop induction-head task.
  We also establish compactness bounds for embedding dimension and numerical precision. Specifically, a compactness theorem shows that any function computable at all can be computed with embedding dimension and precision bounded by the discrete data of the task, namely, head count, alphabet size, and length. Thus, potentially unbounded dimension or precision provably cannot substitute for heads. Finally, we derive nearly matching universal bounds for general binary functions: $2^n$ heads suffice to compute every $n$-bit binary function, with one head per monomial in its multilinear expansion, while a counting argument shows almost all such functions require $Ω(2^n/n^2)$ heads. This lower bound matches the upper bound to within a $\operatorname{poly}(n)$ factor, even when dimension and precision are unbounded. Together, these results characterize head requirements for Boolean computation in this model.

</details>
